Abstract: Given a target dataset, such as faces with eyeglasses, and a background dataset, such as faces without, contrastive analysis separates \textit{salient} factors specific to the target from \textit{common} content shared by both. We aim for salient representations that capture target-specific detail in each image, such as the shape, color, and position of the glasses, so that they reveal subtypes without subtype labels and guide the generation of new examples of a discovered subtype, even one with no name or text description. We introduce SAGE, which learns both factors directly in the high-dimensional spatial latent of a frozen representation autoencoder and conditions a diffusion transformer on the learned salient representation of a reference image. On Digits-ImageNet and FFHQ eyeglasses, SAGE combines high-fidelity \textit{reconstruction} (rFID below $2$) with unsupervised \textit{subtype discovery}, recovering the digits better than baselines (probe accuracy $0.950$ vs.\ at most $0.281$) and revealing eyewear types, finer sunglasses styles, and mislabeled images; salient-conditioned \textit{generation} raises Digits-ImageNet subtype accuracy over the unfactorized latent ($90.5\%$ vs.\ $27.7\%$) and diversity on both datasets. On retinal OCT, SAGE's salient space separates three diseases using only normal/disease labels.

</details>


### [141] [FANVIDv2: Evaluating Video Super-Resolution by Face and Licence-Plate Recognition Under Compound Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39649)
*Kavitha Viswanathan,Vrinda Goel,Shlesh Gholap,Devayan Ghosh,Madhav Gupta,Dhruvi Ganatra,Sanket Potdar,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FANVIDv2视频超分基准，以识别管线在输出上的表现来评分，而非仅用PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VSR用双三次下采样片段上的PSNR/SSIM评判，但在监控场景中真正目标是让人脸与车牌可被识别，两者目标不一致。

Method: 构建包含48位公众人物面部（各1张HR图库图）的320×180 LR片段与375个车牌片段（360个不同车牌串）的基准；LR由随机复合退化（模糊、缩放抖动、传感器噪声、JPEG压缩、最终下采样）生成。提出检测框内识别指标：FaceRecBox要求人脸既被定位又正确识别，TextRecBox按定位质量加权的归一化编辑距离评分。基线为2.3M参数RCDM及残差图门控变体RCDM-RMGF。

Result: RCDM使FaceRecBox由0.6864升至0.7222，匹配人脸的identity accuracy由84.35%升至86.93%，TextRecBox由0.3088升至0.3667；RCDM-RMGF在车牌上达0.3801。

Conclusion: 详细描述了退化模型、基线架构与评分器，并公开标注、元数据、下载与退化脚本及评测代码。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) is normally judged by PSNR and SSIM on clips that were downsampled bicubically, although in surveillance its purpose is to make faces and licence plates \emph{recognisable}. We present FANVIDv2, a benchmark that scores VSR by what a recognition pipeline can do with its output. FANVIDv2 provides $320\times180$ low-resolution (LR) clips with high-resolution (HR) references for 48 public figures (with one HR gallery image each) and 375 licence-plate clips covering 360 distinct plate strings. LR clips are generated with a randomised compound degradation (blur, resize jitter, sensor noise, JPEG compression, final downsampling) rather than bicubic downsampling alone. Two metrics score recognition \emph{inside} detections: FaceRecBox rewards a face only if it is localised and correctly identified, and TextRecBox scores plate transcriptions by normalised edit distance weighted by localisation quality. With a 2.3\,M-parameter VSR baseline (RCDM), FaceRecBox rises from 0.6864 to 0.7222, identity accuracy on matched faces from 84.35\% to 86.93\%, and TextRecBox from 0.3088 to 0.3667; a residual-map gated variant (RCDM-RMGF) reaches 0.3801 on plates. We describe the degradation model, the baseline architectures and the scorers in detail, and release annotations, metadata, download and degradation scripts and evaluation code.

</details>


### [142] [Diffusable Latents from Structure-Agnostic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39657)
*Adrien Ramanana Rahary,Nicolas Dufour,Patrick Pérez,David Picard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将预训练基础模型蒸馏到自编码器瓶颈可提升潜在扩散能力，但标准逐位置蒸馏将潜在布局绑定到教师特征布局。本文指出该约束不必要：仅将单张图像级池化描述子对齐到教师即可达到同等甚至更好效果，并验证了跨潜在形状与教师模态的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准蒸馏逐位置对齐潜在与教师特征，使潜在布局被绑定到教师布局，限制了潜在空间设计的自由度。作者质疑这一位置对齐约束是否必要。

Method: 对比一阶（first-order）与关系型（relational）池化目标，在多种潜在形状与教师模态下进行实验。一阶匹配自然地扩展到一维token序列潜在及跨模态场景；关系型目标仅基于每张图像最近邻。

Result: 将单张图像级池化描述子对齐到教师，效果与逐位置稠密蒸馏相当或略优。一阶匹配可扩展至1D token序列潜在与跨模态，将文本编码器蒸馏到图像自编码器仍提升扩散能力；基于最近邻的关系型目标同样有效。

Conclusion: 位置对齐约束并非必要，结构无关的池化蒸馏是一种更简单且更通用的方案，为潜在扩散模型的自编码器瓶颈设计提供更大灵活性。代码与博客已公开。

Abstract: Distilling pretrained foundation models into an autoencoder bottleneck improves latent diffusability, enabling diffusion models to converge faster and reach higher sample quality. Standard distillation aligns the latent at each position to a co-located teacher feature, tying the latent layout to the teacher's. We show this constraint is unnecessary: aligning a single pooled image-level descriptor to the teacher's performs as well as or slightly better than dense position-wise distillation. We compare first-order and relational pooled objectives across latent shapes and teacher modalities. First-order matching extends naturally to 1D token-sequence latents and across modalities, where distilling a text encoder into an image autoencoder still improves diffusability; a relational objective based only on each image's nearest neighbours improves it as well. Code and blog post are available at https://github.com/AdrienRR/structure-agnostic-distillation and https://kyutai.org/blog/2026-09-28-structure-agnostic-distillation/.

</details>


### [143] [BAM! Bayesian Anything Model: a foundation model for generative computational imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39660)
*Alessio Spagnoletti,Charlesquin Kemajou Mbakam,Jonathan Spence,Andrés Almansa,Marcelo Pereyra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BAM 是一个仅 36M 参数的轻量级贝叶斯基础模型，将 RAM 骨干升级为条件流映射，实现少步、物理感知的后验采样，可零样本或少微调泛化到未见数据与任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式计算成像缺乏物理感知的基础模型：大型基础图像模型作为即插即用先验需零样本近似似然引导，带来显著偏差与计算开销；而物理感知生成模型虽无此偏差，却各自绑定特定数据集、任务和仪器，泛化性差。

Method: BAM 将算子条件化的 Reconstruct Anything Model (RAM) 骨干升级为条件流映射，使仪器物理特性在推理时指定而非训练时固定。模型仅 36M 参数，在大规模图像语料与前向算子库上联合预训练，单个网络即可在少步内完成后验采样，无需似然近似，也无需调节引导权重。

Result: 在 FFHQ、AFHQ、LSUN、DIV2K 及 Kohler 相机抖动基准的线性逆问题上，BAM 仅用 3 步便在样本质量上超越专用模型和领先零样本方法，且计算成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: BAM 为社区提供了进入生成式计算成像的便捷入口，降低了训练成像模型的经济与环境成本，并为物理感知贝叶斯计算成像研究开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Generative models are transforming Bayesian computational imaging, yet the field still lacks physics-aware foundation models. Current practice falls into two camps. Large foundation image models are deployed as plug-and-play priors with zero-shot approximate likelihood guidance, which introduces significant bias and computational cost. Physics-aware generative models avoid this bias, but each is tied to a specific dataset, task and instrument. We introduce BAM (Bayesian Anything Model), a lightweight foundation model for few-step, physics-aware posterior sampling that generalises robustly to unseen data and tasks, zero-shot or with minimal finetuning. BAM upgrades the operator-conditioned Reconstruct Anything Model (RAM) backbone (Terris et al.) into a conditional flow map, so instrument physics is specified at inference time rather than fixed during training. BAM has just 36M parameters and is pre-trained jointly on large image corpora and libraries of forward operators. A single network then draws posterior samples in a few steps, with no likelihood approximation and no guidance weights to tune. Across linear inverse problems on FFHQ, AFHQ, LSUN, DIV2K and the Kohler camera-shake benchmark, BAM outperforms in just 3 steps both specialised models and leading zero-shot methods in sample quality, at a fraction of their computational cost. BAM gives the community an accessible entry point to generative computational imaging, lowers the economic and environmental cost of training imaging models, and opens a new path for research on physics-aware Bayesian computational imaging. Official page: https://bayesian-anything-model.github.io/

</details>


### [144] [Typographic Attack Against VLM-based AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39662)
*Eunmin Lee,Jungwoo Kim,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了排版攻击对视觉语言模型（VLM）检测AI生成图像能力的影响，发现推理模式比直接模式更脆弱，攻击效果存在方向不对称性，且更大的模型虽检测准确率更高但攻击成功率也更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被越来越多地用于AI生成图像检测并能提供自然语言解释，但其识别图像内文字的能力可能使判断暴露于误导性语义线索之下，这一安全隐患尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者在检测专用、开放权重和商业VLM上系统评估排版攻击策略，涵盖"真变假"和"假变真"两个方向，比较推理模式与直接模式的脆弱性，并考察图像与文本变换下的攻击鲁棒性，以及叠加正确类别提示能否帮助纠错。

Result: 推理模式比直接模式更易受攻击；攻击效果存在明显的方向不对称性；更大的模型清洁检测准确率更高，但攻击成功率也更高；研究还检验了图像/文本变换下的鲁棒性及叠加正确类别标签的纠错效果。

Conclusion: 这些分析刻画了排版攻击如何影响真实性判断，并揭示了当前基于VLM的AI生成图像检测系统的局限性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for AI-generated image (AIGI) detection, providing natural-language explanations for authenticity judgments. However, their ability to interpret text within images may also expose these judgments to misleading semantic cues. We systematically evaluate typographic attack strategies across detection-oriented, open-weight, and commercial VLMs, considering both real-to-fake and fake-to-real attacks. Our results show that reasoning modes generally exhibit greater vulnerability than direct modes and that attack effectiveness exhibits pronounced directional asymmetry. Moreover, larger models tend to exhibit higher clean detection accuracy but also higher attack success rates. We further examine attack robustness under image and text transformations and investigate whether overlays indicating the correct class can aid error correction. Together, these analyses characterize how typographic attacks influence authenticity judgments and expose limitations of current VLM-based AIGI detection systems.

</details>


### [145] [MC-PanDA++: Simpler, Stronger, and More Robust Domain-Adaptive Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39681)
*Ivan Martinović,Josip Šarić,Yuki M. Asano,Siniša Šegvić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MC-PanDA++ 通过自监督视觉编码器、逐类别自适应掩码损失缩放和单阶段训练流程，改进了域自适应全景分割方法，使其更简单、性能更强、更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督域自适应（UDA）可减少全景分割的标注负担。然而现有全景 UDA 方法多基于次优的逐像素分割架构，而先进的掩码 Transformer 因在一致性学习中易受确认偏差影响（错误教师预测被强化）而很少被采用。作者此前的 MC-PanDA 通过细粒度置信度估计缓解此问题，但存在训练流程复杂、超参数调优繁琐的缺陷。

Method: MC-PanDA++ 引入三项改进：(i) 自监督视觉编码器，提供更强更鲁棒的初始化，进一步减少对人类标注的依赖；(ii) 逐类别、自适应掩码级损失缩放，稳定训练并实现跨域使用单一超参数集；(iii) 单阶段训练流程，降低整体概念复杂度。

Result: 相比 MC-PanDA，MC-PanDA++ 概念上更简单、性能更好、更鲁棒，同时降低了训练复杂度与超参数调优负担。

Conclusion: MC-PanDA++ 为域自适应全景分割提供了一种更简洁、更高效且更鲁棒的方法。源代码已公开于 https://github.com/martinovicivan/MC-PanDA。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) reduces the annotation burden in panoptic segmentation by leveraging a cost-effectively labeled source domain (e.g., synthetic) and an unlabeled target domain to bridge the distribution gap. Existing panoptic UDA methods rely on teacher-student consistency learning built upon suboptimal per-pixel segmentation architectures. In contrast, state-of-the-art mask transformers are rarely adopted due to their pronounced vulnerability to confirmation bias in consistency learning, where erroneous teacher predictions are reinforced during training. Our earlier approach, MC-PanDA, mitigates this issue through fine-grained confidence estimation, which suppresses gradients from unreliable masks while sampling informative yet reliable locations for loss computation. However, this method entails a complex multi-stage training and requires careful hyperparameter tuning. This work presents MC-PanDA++, which addresses these limitations by introducing: (i) self-supervised vision encoders that provide a stronger and more robust initialization, further reducing the reliance on human annotations, (ii) per-class, self-adapting mask-wide loss scaling that stabilizes training and enables the usage of a single set of hyperparameters across domains, and (iii) a single-stage training pipeline that decreases overall conceptual complexity. Together, these improvements result in a conceptually simpler, better-performing, and more robust method for domain-adaptive panoptics. Source code: https://github.com/martinovicivan/MC-PanDA

</details>


### [146] [Unapologetically Distributed: A Call for Decentralized Document Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39684)
*Adrià Molina,Oriol Ramos Terrades,Josep Lladós*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Unapologetically Distributed"，首次同时从任务、架构和微调策略三个维度系统评估文档分析中的分布式学习，证明去中心化不仅不是性能妥协，反而能提升模型泛化与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档分析领域隐私问题日益突出，档案机构、政府机构和本地企业在法律与政策限制下难以采用自动化方案。联邦学习常被视为"必要的恶"，被认为必须牺牲性能以换取去中心化与隐私，而许多研究忽视了它在提升分布外数据鲁棒性方面的潜力。

Method: 提出"Unapologetically Distributed"，首次从三个关键维度（所处理的任务、所采用的架构、所应用的微调策略）同时评估分布式学习在文档分析中的表现；在多种分布式训练方法下，针对表格识别、手写识别和词定位等任务进行实验，特别关注迁移学习阶段。

Result: 实验表明，各类分布式训练方法在表格识别、手写识别和词定位等多样任务中均能增强泛化能力，尤其是在迁移学习阶段效果显著。

Conclusion: 去中心化并非单纯的约束条件，而是提升真实文档分析场景下模型鲁棒性与适应性的宝贵机会，为隐私受限环境下的文档分析自动化提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Privacy has become an increasingly important concern in the Document Analysis community, to the extent that in many environments such as archives, governmental institutions, and local businesses, the adoption of automation is restricted by legal and policy constraints. While federated learning has often been regarded as a ``necessary evil'', implying an unavoidable performance trade-off in exchange for decentralization and privacy, many prior works overlook its potential to improve robustness to out-of-distribution data. In this paper, we present Unapologetically Distributed, the first comprehensive study evaluating distributed learning in Document Analysis along three key axes simultaneously: the tasks addressed, the architectures employed, and the fine-tuning strategies applied. Specifically, we demonstrate how various distributed training approaches enhance generalization capabilities across diverse tasks such as Table Recognition, handwriting recognition, and Word Spotting, particularly during transfer learning stages. Our results provide strong evidence that decentralization is not merely a constraint, but a valuable opportunity to improve model robustness and adaptability in real-world Document Analysis scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [When Masking Helps or Hurts Robustness in Compressed CLIP: A Pre-Deployment Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39704)
*Muhammad Zawish,Steven Davy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究语义掩码（semantic masking）对最差组鲁棒性的影响，发现其效果高度不稳定，并提出无标签的预部署诊断指标SIM来预测其效果，同时设计了高效的GPU分割例程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于掩码的token剪枝对最差组鲁棒性是帮助还是损害，此前无法在部署前预测。作者希望对语义掩码在不同虚假相关基准上的效果进行系统研究，并找到一种无需标签、无需微调的预部署诊断方法。

Method: 在8个虚假相关基准上系统研究语义掩码；发现效果不稳定源于"虚假反转"（spurious inversion）现象；提出Spurious Inversion Metric (SIM)，一种无标签、预部署的诊断指标；并设计批量、无同步的GPU分割例程以降低运行时开销。

Result: 语义掩码可使最差组准确率相对提升高达82.5%，也可使其下降高达100%。SIM的符号在所有8个数据集上以统计显著性（二项检验p=0.035）预测该效果，并在6种CLIP架构上保持可靠。朴素掩码是平均准确率损失最大的方法，其分割步骤是运行时瓶颈。新GPU分割例程将开销从3.5倍降至1.75倍。按SIM符号门控部署，在8个数据集中的7个上匹配或超过强剪枝基线。

Conclusion: 基于掩码的token剪枝效果不稳定，可通过SIM在部署前预测；按SIM符号门控部署能获得掩码收益同时避免最差失败。

Abstract: This paper demonstrate that whether masking-based token pruning helps or hurts worst-group robustness can be predicted before deployment, without labels or fine-tuning. A systematic study of semantic masking across 8 spurious-correlation benchmarks shows its effect on worst-group accuracy is highly unstable: it improves accuracy by up to 82.5\% relative on some datasets and degrades it by up to 100\% on others. We trace this instability to spurious inversion: background patches receive higher CLIP text-similarity than the true object when the spurious attribute is background-separable, inverting the assumption every text- and attention-guided pruning method relies on. We introduce the Spurious Inversion Metric (SIM), a label-free, pre-deployment diagnostic whose sign predicts this effect with statistical significance (binomial $p=0.035$) across all 8 datasets, and remains dependable across 6 CLIP architectures with a clean foreground/background split. Naive masking is itself a major source of risk: it causes the largest average-accuracy loss of any method we evaluate, and its own per-image segmentation step is a significant runtime bottleneck. To address this, we design a batched, synchronization-free GPU segmentation routine that cuts this overhead from 3.5$\times$ to 1.75$\times$ baseline. Gating deployment by SIM's sign recovers masking's benefits while avoiding its worst failures, matching or exceeding a strong pruning baseline on 7 of 8 datasets.

</details>


### [148] [BTC3D: Blended Tile Conditioning for Detail-Enhancing Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39709)
*Junyu Li,Qiuyu Chen,Pengcheng Wang,Shiqi Yang,Alexandra Gomez-Villa,Joost van de Weijer,Ruilin Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BTC3D，一种免训练、推理时的图像到3D生成框架，通过混合分块嵌入与动态条件调度增强细节保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像到3D方法多采用全局编码条件特征，压缩了空间信息，导致"细节衰减"，难以复现细粒度细节；而提升质量通常需重训或微调大模型，计算成本高、不实用。

Method: 首先分析图像到3D模型中的图像特征可加性，据此引入混合分块嵌入，从切分的图像区域块中提取局部条件信号；并提出动态条件调度，在扩散后期低噪声阶段逐步增大分块级条件的影响，以稳定融合全局与局部引导。方法完全在推理时运行，可无缝集成到现有图像到3D扩散流程。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在免训练条件下显著提升基础模型的纹理质量与视觉保真度，同时保持全局结构一致性。

Conclusion: BTC3D为图像到3D生成提供了一种免训练、可即插即用的细节增强方案，兼顾局部细节与全局结构。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based pipelines have achieved promising progress in image-to-3D synthesis. However, generating high-fidelity details remains challenging, especially when the input image contains rich details. Existing approaches often rely on globally encoded conditioning features, which compress spatial information and limit the model to reproduce fine-grained details. This common design often leads to a phenomenon we term detail attenuation. Moreover, improving image-to-3D synthesis quality typically requires retraining or fine-tuning large diffusion models, which can be computationally expensive and impractical for complex 3D pipelines. In this work, we present Blended Tile Conditioning for image-to-3D generation (BTC3D), a training-free inference time framework that enhances fine-grained detail preservation in image-to-3D diffusion pipelines. To alleviate detail attenuation, we first examine the image feature additivity in image-to-3D models. Based on this property, we introduce a blended tile embedding that extracts local conditioning signals from split image regional patches, allowing the diffusion model to better preserve fine-grained visual details. To integrate the global and local conditioning guidance stably, we propose a dynamic conditioning schedule that gradually increases the influence of tile-level conditioning during later low-noise stages of diffusion. Our proposed method BTC3D operates entirely at inference time and can be seamlessly integrated into existing image-to-3D diffusion pipelines. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves texture quality and visual fidelity of the base model while maintaining global structural consistency in a training-free manner.

</details>


### [149] [Let the Carrier Carry the Attack: Preserving the Subject in Adversarial Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39723)
*Linfeng Jiang,Steven McDonagh,Yuhang Chen,Xingyu Zhao,Siddartha Khastgir,Andi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"载体"(carrier)概念，即在图像中引入一个次要视觉元素作为辅助区域，在全局分类器引导下进行对抗攻击，从而在保持攻击强度的同时保护主体完整性并提升跨模型迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强无限制对抗攻击往往会扭曲图像的主要对象（主体），破坏其完整性。作者希望在不过度牺牲攻击强度的前提下，缓解主体失真问题，同时提升攻击的迁移性和主体保留效果。

Method: 引入"载体"(carrier)——一个次要视觉元素，为攻击提供辅助空间区域。在全局分类器引导下，让载体吸收更大份额的全局归一化攻击更新，从而分散对主体的扰动。研究还涉及目标相关特征的强度，用以平衡语义分离与迁移性能。

Result: 1. 载体通过吸收更大份额的全局归一化攻击更新，缓解主体失真；2. 载体提升跨模型迁移性，该性能由平衡语义分离与迁移性能的目标相关特征强度决定；3. 成功的定向攻击能保留个性化主体作为人类感知的主要内容，同时成功误导分类器。

Conclusion: 视觉上次要的载体为对抗扰动提供了一条辅助空间通路，使攻击既强又具迁移性，同时提升主体保留度。

Abstract: Strong unrestricted adversarial attacks can distort the primary object of an image, hereafter referred to as the subject. To preserve subject integrity without compromising attack magnitude, we introduce the carrier: a secondary visual element that provides an auxiliary region to facilitate the attack under global classifier guidance. We demonstrate three key findings: 1. A carrier mitigates subject distortion by absorbing a larger share of globally normalized attack updates. 2. A carrier improves cross-model transferability, governed by the strength of target-related features that balance semantic separation and transfer performance. 3. Successful targeted attacks retain the personalized subject as the primary content perceived by humans while successfully misleading the classifier. Our results demonstrate that a visually secondary carrier offers an auxiliary spatial pathway for adversarial changes, enabling strong and transferable attacks while improving subject preservation.

</details>


### [150] [FAST: Flow Any Scene Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39748)
*Yongjian Zhang,Longguang Wang,Zhuo Song,Zhiheng Fu,Liang Lin,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FAST（Flow Any Scene Transformer），一种可扩展的对应匹配模型，通过复用单视图视觉基础模型的查询-键投影先验，实现跨视图密集匹配，并构建600万图像对训练语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言与视觉基础模型的进展主要由规模扩展驱动，但规模扩展在精确对应匹配（correspondence matching）中的作用尚未被充分探索。现有ViT匹配器通常需要专门的"成对为中心"的预训练阶段，限制了其随单视图基础模型进步而扩展的能力。

Method: 基于两点洞察：1）单视图视觉基础模型内部的查询-键投影编码了粗粒度但可复用的跨视图匹配先验；2）以交叉注意力形式复用这些预训练投影，可为基于单视图编码器构建的ViT匹配器提供高效初始化。据此，FAST以普通单视图基础模型为骨干，采用零参数重连策略（zero-parameter rewiring），将选定的自注意力层转换为交叉注意力以进行跨视图交互，从而无需专门的成对预训练。同时构建了600万图像对的通用密集2D位移估计训练语料。

Result: 大量实验表明，FAST在广泛基准上达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能，并且在骨干网络规模和训练数据量两方面均表现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: FAST证明单视图基础模型的内部注意力投影可作为跨视图匹配的有效先验，借助零参数重连即可构建可扩展的对应匹配模型，使其能随单视图基础模型的进步而受益，并绕开专门的成对预训练阶段。

Abstract: Scaling has become a primary driver of progress in language and vision foundation models, yet its role in precise correspondence matching remains underexplored. In this work, we present Flow Any Scene Transformer (FAST), a scalable correspondence model driven by two key insights. First, we reveal that the query-key projections inside single-view vision foundation models encode a coarse yet reusable prior for cross-view matching. Second, reusing these pretrained projections in cross-attention form yields a highly effective initialization for a ViT-based matcher built from a single-view encoder. Guided by these insights, we build FAST upon a vanilla single-view foundation model, utilizing a zero-parameter rewiring strategy to convert selected self-attention layers into cross-attention for cross-view interaction. This design allows ViT-based matchers to scale with advances in single-view foundation models, bypassing the need for a dedicated pair-centric pretraining stage. To fully unlock the scaling potential of this formulation, we assemble a 6-million-pair training corpus for general-purpose dense 2D displacement estimation across diverse co-visible image pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FAST achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of benchmarks, while scaling favorably with both backbone size and training data.

</details>


### [151] [Determining Vertical Displacement of Agricultural Areas Using UAV-Photogrammetry and a Heteroscedastic Deep Learning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39756)
*Wojciech Gruszczyński,Edyta Puniach,Paweł Ćwiąkała,Wojciech Matwij*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于U-Net的异方差回归算法，从UAV摄影测量点云直接估算垂直地表位移，在多数测试中略优于传统地面滤波方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面滤波依赖点云分类，植被对地面高程估计干扰大，因此需要一种能减少植被影响并量化不确定性的替代方法。

Method: 采用U-Net架构进行异方差回归，预测高程改正的条件期望值以削弱植被影响，同时估计高程改正方差的对数以量化不确定性；使用RMSE、确定位移节点百分比和异常值百分比三项指标，通过TOPSIS法在四个数据集上与多种地面滤波算法对比。

Result: 多数情况下U-Net方法略优于传统地面滤波。以一个测试数据集为例，U-Net的沉降RMSE为6.1 cm，确定沉降节点占80.5%，异常值占0.2%；而次优的SMRF模型RMSE为7.7 cm，确定节点占77.3%，异常值占0.3%。

Conclusion: U-Net异方差回归为UAV点云垂直位移测定提供了一种可行且具竞争力的替代方案，并能同时给出位移值的不确定性估计。

Abstract: This article introduces an algorithm that uses a U-Net architecture to determine vertical ground surface displacements from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-photogrammetry point clouds, offering an alternative to traditional ground filtering methods. Unlike con-ventional ground filters that rely on point cloud classification, the proposed approach em-ploys heteroscedastic regression. The U-Net model predicts the conditional expected val-ues of the elevation corrections, aiming to reduce the impact of vegetation on determined ground surface elevations. Concurrently, it estimates the logarithm of the elevation cor-rection variance, allowing for direct quantification of the uncertainty associated with each elevation correction value. The algorithm was evaluated using three metrics: the root mean square error (RMSE) of vertical displacements, the percentage of nodes with deter-mined displacement values, and the percentage of outliers among those values. Perfor-mance was assessed using the technique for order of preference by similarity to ideal so-lution (TOPSIS) method and compared against several ground-filter-based algorithms across four datasets, each including at least two time intervals. In most cases, the U-Net-based approach demonstrated a slight performance advantage over traditional ground filtering techniques. For example, for the U-Net-based algorithm, for one of the test da-tasets, the RMSE of the determined subsidences was 6.1 cm, the percentage of nodes with determined subsidences was 80.5%, and the percentage of outliers was 0.2%. For the same case, the algorithm based on the next best model (SMRF) allowed an RMSE of 7.7 cm to be obtained; for 77.3% of nodes, the subsidences were determined; and the percentage of outliers was 0.3%.

</details>


### [152] [Seeing as Humans Do: Learning from Motion to Segment Anything Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39785)
*Weijian Jian,Xiaoyue Zhang,Bin Xiao,Chunyu Xie,Yixiao He,Yutao Liu,Dawei Leng,Yuhui Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSA：从无标注视频中学习可迁移的物体先验，实现零标注、可扩展的"分割万物"框架，性能接近全监督SAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM依赖海量人工标注，构成模型扩展的根本瓶颈；而无监督方法从运动中学物体概念时往往过拟合运动实体，缺乏多粒度理解且无法泛化到静态物体。

Method: 提出MoSA三阶段框架：(1) 从大规模视频自动生成多粒度运动伪标签；(2) 通过对比学习训练感知分组模型（PGM），内化泛化的、以外观驱动的物体概念；(3) 将该先验迁移到提示引导架构中，实现类SAM的图像推理。

Result: 在COCO、ADE20K等七个挑战性基准上零样本评测，显著优于现有无监督方法；在完全无人工标注的情况下，分割性能与全监督SAM相当。

Conclusion: 利用大规模无标注运动是替代标注驱动分割流程的可行且高可扩展方案。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model (SAM) relies heavily on massive manual annotations, creating a fundamental bottleneck for model scaling. While unsupervised methods attempt to learn object concepts from motion, they typically overfit to moving entities, lacking both multi-granularity understanding and the ability to generalize to static objects. To overcome this, we introduce Motion-Grounded Segment Anything (MoSA), a highly scalable unsupervised framework that learns a transferable objectness prior from unlabeled videos. MoSA operates in three progressive stages: (1) automatically generating multi-granularity motion pseudo-labels from large-scale video data; (2) training a Perceptual Grouping Model (PGM) via contrastive learning to internalize a generalized, appearance-driven concept of objects; and (3) transferring this learned prior into a prompt-guided architecture for segment-anything-style inference on images. Extensive zero-shot evaluations across seven challenging benchmarks (e.g., COCO and ADE20K) demonstrate that MoSA significantly outperforms existing unsupervised methods. Notably, despite using zero manual annotations, MoSA achieves segmentation performance comparable to the fully supervised SAM. Our findings reveal that harnessing large-scale unlabeled motion is a feasible and highly scalable alternative to annotation-driven segment-anything pipelines.

</details>


### [153] [Inline Memory Meets Reusable Skills: Memory-centric Framework for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39794)
*Zaijing Li,Rui Shao,Bing Hu,Haoyu Zhang,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Optimus-R，一种以记忆为中心的VLA框架，将机器人适应形式化为显式的查询-技能记忆调优，实现数据高效技能学习并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型适应新任务和领域效率低下：多依赖参数微调，成本高且易导致对已学任务的灾难性遗忘。

Method: 包含三部分：(i) 内联记忆接口用于技能提取，向VLA前缀流插入可学习记忆token，使主干在原生动作条件路径中导出控制感知的查询与技能表示；(ii) 查询-技能记忆库用于技能学习，将技能外化为决定"取什么"的查询原型和指定"如何行动"的技能值，支持有限参数更新下的技能复用与扩展；(iii) 轻量Bridge-and-Adapt机制用于技能更新，通过轻量适配器和残差记忆更新对齐目标域查询与技能。

Result: 在域内适应、跨域适应和终身学习实验中，Optimus-R实现数据高效的技能学习，同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 以记忆为中心的查询-技能记忆调优是VLA模型高效适应的有效途径，兼顾技能学习效率与旧任务保持能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet adapting them to new tasks and domains remains inefficient: existing methods often rely on parameter tuning, incurring substantial costs and risking catastrophic forgetting of previously learned tasks. To address this, we propose \textbf{Optimus-R}, a memory-centric VLA framework that formulates robotic adaptation as explicit query-skill memory tuning. Optimus-R introduces: (i) An \textbf{Inline Memory Interface for skill extraction}. It inserts learnable memory tokens into the VLA prefix stream, allowing the backbone to derive control-aware query and skill representations within the native action-conditioning pathway. (ii) A \textbf{Query-Skill Memory Bank for skill learning}. It externalizes skills into query prototypes for deciding \emph{what} to retrieve and skill values for specifying \emph{how} to act, supporting skill reuse and expansion with limited parameter updates. (iii) A lightweight \textbf{Bridge-and-Adapt mechanism for skill updating}. It aligns target-domain queries and skills with the existing memory space through a lightweight adapter and residual memory updates. Experiments on in-domain adaptation, cross-domain adaptation, and lifelong learning show that Optimus-R enables data-efficient skill learning while mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [154] [P-SRM: Selective Recovery of Rejected Predictions in Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39832)
*Youbin He,Siwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 P-SRM（拒绝后选择性恢复方法），利用空间响应、历史接受状态和原生决策裕度重新评估被拒绝的候选，选择性地恢复可靠预测，从而提升跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉跟踪中的拒绝机制在抑制不可靠预测的同时，也会误拒正确局部化的候选，导致有用信息被浪费。作者希望识别并恢复这些被误拒的候选，同时保持原生接受输出与候选坐标不变。

Method: 提出 P-SRM 方法，结合空间响应、过去已接受状态和原生决策裕度，对被拒绝的候选重新评估并进行选择性恢复。在六个跟踪器和四个数据集上评估，覆盖类别特定、点和通用目标跟踪，共九种配置。代码已开源。

Result: 在全部九种配置中，P-SRM 均改善了被拒绝候选的排序及整体跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，拒绝后验证能够识别并恢复被原生拒绝机制丢弃的有用预测，证明了重用被拒绝信息的价值。

Abstract: Many visual tracking methods use rejection mechanisms to suppress unreliable predictions. However, these mechanisms can also reject correctly localized candidates, leaving useful information unused. We investigate how to identify and recover these candidates while preserving native accepted outputs and candidate coordinates. To this end, we propose P-SRM (Post-rejection Selective Recovery Method), which combines spatial responses, past accepted states, and native decision margins to reassess candidates and selectively restore reliable predictions. We evaluate P-SRM on six trackers and four datasets spanning category-specific, point, and generic object tracking. Across all nine configurations, P-SRM improves rejected-candidate ranking and overall tracking performance. These results show that post-rejection verification can identify and recover useful predictions discarded by native rejection, demonstrating the value of reusing rejected information. Project repository: https://github.com/PalestyHR/P-SRM.

</details>


### [155] [Spherical Interpolation for Backward-Compatible Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39836)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究对比视觉-语言模型升级时的后向兼容问题，提出在对齐后于两个归一化查询表示之间的球面测地线上做插值（SLERP），无需重建图库索引即可提升检索性能，并提供几何刻画与基于间隔的Recall@K认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的视觉-语言模型产生互不兼容的表示空间，替换已部署模型通常需对全图库重新计算嵌入，代价极高。现有正交后处理对齐只能近似地把新模型查询映射到旧模型图库空间，仍存在残留角度偏差。

Method: 在旧模型查询与对齐后新模型查询两个归一化表示之间的球面测地线上进行插值。刻画该路径何时存在比两端更接近理想检索最优方向的内部查询方向，并通过局部间隔（margin）认证将该刻画与Recall@K联系起来。

Result: 在多个基准和模型家族上的实验表明，对齐后的球面插值优于单独的正交对齐，在多数评测设置中恢复后向兼容性；与几何刻画一致，逐查询oracle分析显示检索有利的内部点在实践中频繁出现。

Conclusion: 球面测地线插值是一种无需重建索引、有效恢复模型升级后向兼容性的后处理方案。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models map visual and textual representations into a shared normalized embedding space, making cosine similarity the natural metric for cross-modal retrieval. A practical challenge arises during model upgrades: independently trained models generally produce incompatible representation spaces, so replacing a deployed model typically requires recomputing embeddings for the entire gallery, which is prohibitively expensive at scale. Orthogonal post-hoc alignment can partially mitigate this problem by mapping new-model queries into the old-model gallery space. However, because independently trained models can differ in fine-grained representation structure, the orthogonal alignment remains approximate, leaving a residual angular discrepancy between the old-model query and the aligned new-model query. We study whether interpolation along the spherical geodesic between these two normalized query representations can improve retrieval without re-indexing the gallery. We characterize when this path contains an interior query direction closer to an idealized retrieval-optimal direction than either endpoint, and connect this characterization to Recall@$K$ through a local margin-based certification result. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and model families show that post-alignment spherical interpolation improves over orthogonal alignment alone, recovering backward-compatibility in most evaluated settings. Consistent with our geometric characterization, per-query oracle analysis shows that retrieval-favorable interior points occur frequently in practice. Code is available at https://github.com/miccunifi/SLERP_backward_compatibility .

</details>


### [156] [DyRAD: Radar Novel View Synthesis for Dynamic Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39841)
*Merav Keidar,Tomer Borreda,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DyRAD提出一种用于动态驾驶场景的雷达新视角合成方法，将场景分解为静态背景反射体与运动跟踪的动态反射体，渲染完整的距离-方位-多普勒（RAD）张量，实现零样本传感器配置迁移，并在多数据集上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达新视角合成未能利用多普勒测速能力：处理动态场景的方法仅重建距离-方位张量，而渲染多普勒的方法假设场景静态。此外，雷达信号处理会把单个反射扩散到多个bin，现有表示将此扩散吸收进场景几何，导致视角移动时渲染错误。

Method: DyRAD用静态背景反射体与运动跟踪的动态点反射体建模动态驾驶场景，渲染完整RAD张量。反射体速度由目标轨迹导出并投影到视线方向，使多普勒既作为渲染输出又作为轨迹监督。反射体通过由雷达信号处理链导出的固定解析点扩散函数（PSF）渲染，防止传感器引起的扩散被固化到场景表示中。

Result: 在RADIal、Boreas及合成基准上，覆盖原始轨迹位姿与位移视角评估。在RADIal上，DyRAD在90.7%的参考检测目标中恢复雷达检测，而最强基线仅为26.9%。分离PSF还实现了零样本传感器配置迁移。

Conclusion: DyRAD通过解耦传感器扩散与场景表示并引入多普勒监督，提升了动态雷达场景重建质量，并支持无需重新拟合的跨雷达规格渲染，为自动驾驶闭环评估提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic driving scenes from recorded sensor data supports closed-loop evaluation of autonomous driving systems by synthesizing observations beyond the original trajectory. Unlike cameras and LiDAR, radar measures radial velocity directly through Doppler. Yet existing radar novel-view synthesis fails to exploit this capability: methods addressing dynamic scenes reconstruct only range-azimuth tensors, while methods that render Doppler assume static scenes. Moreover, because radar processing spreads each reflection across multiple bins, existing representations absorb this spread into scene geometry, causing it to render incorrectly when the viewpoint moves. We present DyRAD, which models dynamic driving scenes using static background reflectors and motion-tracked dynamic point reflectors to render complete range-azimuth-Doppler (RAD) tensors. Reflector velocities are derived from object tracks and projected onto the line of sight, making Doppler both a rendered output and supervision for those tracks. Crucially, we render reflectors through a fixed analytic point-spread function (PSF) derived from the radar's signal-processing chain, preventing sensor-induced spread from being baked into the scene representation. Beyond improving scene reconstruction, this separation also enables zero-shot sensor-configuration transfer, allowing the same reconstructed scene to be rendered under different radar specifications without refitting. We evaluate DyRAD on RADIal, Boreas, and a synthetic benchmark across both on-path poses and displaced viewpoints untested by prior work. On RADIal, DyRAD recovers radar detections in 90.7% of reference-detected objects, compared with 26.9% for the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [157] [Hyperspectral Image Models: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39871)
*Tanishq Rachamalla,Aryan Das,Srishti Kaushik,Swalpa Kumar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hyperspectral Image Models 模块化框架，统一55个高光谱模型、24个基准场景与标准化评估流程，经1320次模型-场景评测发现场景难度主导性能，无单一范式普遍最优，极小模型可媲美超大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱遥感虽已涵盖CNN、ViT、Mamba、GNN、KAN、自监督掩码自编码等多种深度学习范式，但研究进展受限于代码库分散、张量约定不兼容、评估标准不统一等问题，缺乏可比、可复现的统一基准。

Method: 构建模块化框架，以统一注册表整合六大范式55个代表性模型，支持4D/5D张量自动适配与标准化构造器；集成机载、星载、无人机及火星CRISM共24个基准场景，提供缓存、标签重映射、PCA、波段选择、空间池化与任意PxP切片；采用类别均衡随机划分和带切比雪夫保护带的空间不相交区域分块，消除训练-测试像素重叠；实验基于单一config.yaml、确定性种子与完整溯源生成LaTeX基准表和分类图。

Result: 在1320次模型-场景评测与6600次带种子运行中，场景难度比架构影响更大：平均精度从Botswana的96.40%到Houston 2018的56.70%，而各范式均值差距约15个百分点；无任何范式普遍占优，参数量低于1M的模型可媲美大两个数量级的架构。

Conclusion: 该框架通过统一模型、数据与评估流程，为高光谱图像分类提供了标准化、可复现的基准平台，并揭示场景难度与架构选择同等重要、模型规模并非决定性能的唯一因素。代码已公开。

Abstract: Hyperspectral remote sensing has advanced across diverse deep learning paradigms, including spectral spatial CNNs, Vision Transformers, Mamba, graph neural networks, Kolmogorov Arnold networks, and self supervised masked autoencoding. Yet progress remains hindered by fragmented repositories, incompatible tensor conventions, and non standardized evaluation. Hyperspectral Image Models addresses these challenges through a modular framework unifying 55 representative models across six paradigms with a common registry, automatic 4D/5D tensor adaptation, and standardized constructors. It integrates 24 benchmark scenes from Airborne, Spaceborne, UAV, and Mars CRISM sensors, with caching, label remapping, PCA, explicit band selection or raw spectra, optional spatial max pooling, and arbitrary PxP patch extraction. To prevent inflated accuracy from overlapping windows, it supports class balanced random partitioning and spatially disjoint regional blocking with Chebyshev guard bands that eliminate train test pixel overlap. Experiments use a single config.yaml with deterministic seeds and complete provenance, generating LaTeX benchmark tables and classification maps. Across 1,320 model scene evaluations and 6,600 seeded runs, scene difficulty dominates architecture, with mean accuracy ranging from 96.40% on Botswana to 56.70% on Houston 2018, versus a 15 point spread across paradigm means. No paradigm universally dominates, while sub 1 M parameter models can match architectures two orders of magnitude larger. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tanishq251/Hyperspectral-Image-Models.

</details>


### [158] [Grounding with Confidence: Controllable Generative Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39883)
*Jinhao Chen,Benlei Cui,Ruijian Jia,Ziheng Wang,Tianyu Wo,Pengfei Sun,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Yitong Yang,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在视频时间定位的生成式模型中引入轻量置信度头，对解码过程中的候选区间进行显式打分，实现候选生成与接受的分离，从而支持排序、阈值选择与拒绝，无需在推理时调用外部验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频时间定位模型通常只输出时间戳，缺乏区间级别的显式置信度分数来指导候选选择。这限制了下游应用对精度-召回偏好的灵活调整。

Method: 将候选生成与接受分离，在原始解码过程中对单个区间打分。具体包括：1) 轻量置信度头读取池化解码器状态，提供用于区间选择的显式分数；2) 用离线验证器分数在固定候选序列上监督该头；3) 在强化学习中用时间重叠标签使其适应当前rollout；4) 用GT锚定的候选池监督与集合级优化训练生成器。

Result: 在固定OMTG-Bench候选池上，相比生成顺序，置信度在10%全局返回预算下将query-macro Recall@0.5从9.95%提升至14.42%，在25%预算下从26.48%提升至31.12%。

Conclusion: 连续置信度分数使下游应用可在不重新生成候选区间的情况下，调整返回预算或接受阈值以匹配自身的精度-召回偏好；分数支持排序、阈值选择与拒绝，且推理时无需外部验证器。

Abstract: Video temporal grounding supports applications such as video search, content review, and automated editing by localizing events described in natural language. Yet existing generative models typically output timestamps without explicit interval-level confidence scores to guide candidate selection. We separate candidate generation from acceptance by scoring individual intervals within the original decoding pass. A lightweight confidence head reads pooled decoder states, providing an explicit score trained for interval selection. Offline verifier scores supervise the head on fixed candidate sequences, and temporal-overlap labels adapt it to current rollouts during reinforcement learning. GT-anchored candidate-pool supervision and set-level optimization train the generator. The resulting scores support ranking, threshold-based selection, and rejection without invoking an external verifier at inference. On a fixed OMTG-Bench candidate pool, confidence raises query-macro Recall@0.5 from 9.95% to 14.42% over generation order at a 10% global return budget, and from 26.48% to 31.12% at a 25% budget. The continuous scores let downstream applications adjust return budgets or acceptance thresholds to match their precision-recall preferences, without regenerating candidate intervals.

</details>


### [159] [Spatial-Temporal Multi-scale Network for Screen Content Video Quality Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39894)
*Ziyin Huang,Sik-Ho Tsang,Xinyuan Qin,Yui-Lam Chan,Xueling Zhou,Feiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出STP-Net，一个专门用于压缩屏幕内容视频增强的时空多尺度网络，通过三个互补模块应对SCV的突变运动、场景切换和高频细节挑战，实验优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕内容视频（SCV）具有突变运动、场景切换和文字图形等高频率细节的特点，与自然视频不同。传统视频增强方法高度依赖时间连续性，在处理SCV时因时间相关性被破坏而性能下降。

Method: 提出时空多尺度网络（STM-Net），包含三个互补组件：先验引导的时空调度器（PG-STD），将输入路由到三个并行流以避免特征污染；双向时间特征提取（BTFE）模块，无需显式检测即可自适应处理突变过渡；级联多尺度特征蒸馏（CMFD）模块，保留关键高频细节。

Result: 实验结果表明，STM-Net在客观指标和主观视觉质量上均优于当前最先进的方法，为屏幕内容伪影提供了稳健的解决方案。代码已开源。

Conclusion: STM-Net通过针对性设计有效解决了压缩屏幕内容视频增强中的时间相关性问题，成为该领域性能领先的稳健方案。

Abstract: Different from natural videos, Screen Content Videos (SCVs) are characterized by abrupt motion, scene switches, and high-frequency details such as text and graphics. Conventional video enhancement methods, which rely heavily on temporal continuity, often suffer from performance degradation when processing SCVs due to the disruption of temporal correlations. To address these challenges, we propose the Spatial-Temporal Multi-scale Network (STM-Net), a novel framework specifically tailored for compressed SCV enhancement. Our approach integrates three complementary components: a Prior-Guided Spatio-Temporal Dispatcher (PG-STD) that routes input into three parallel streams to avoid feature contamination, a Bidirectional Temporal Feature Extraction (BTFE) module that adaptively handles abrupt transitions without explicit detection, and a Cascaded Multi-scale Feature Distillation (CMFD) module that preserves critical high-frequency details. Experimental results demonstrate that STM-Net outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both objective metrics and subjective visual quality, providing a robust solution for screen content artifacts. Code is available at https://github.com/HUANGZiyin1/STM-Net.

</details>


### [160] [Learning Where to Look: Anatomical Grounding and Guided Attention for Cardiac MRI Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39899)
*Bangwei Guo,Xiao Chen,Boris Mailhe,Jia Yao,Yiqing Wang,Ankush Mukherjee,Yikang Liu,Zheyuan Zhang,Hang Yu,Terrence Chen,Shanhui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模CMR解剖定位与临床问答数据集，并提出心脏解剖路由注意力(CARA)方法，形成CARA-VL模型，在临床评估与区域定位上表现优异并具备外部泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振(CMR)图像解读需要识别心脏结构并聚焦于与临床问题相关的区域，这促使了按解剖引导的视觉语言模型(VLM)的发展。然而，针对CMR的解剖定位与临床问答监督数据仍然有限。

Method: 作者构建了128,915个解剖定位对和42,799个临床问答对，覆盖短轴电影、延迟钆增强和长轴电影序列，无需为每张训练图像配对报告。并提出心脏解剖路由注意力(CARA)，根据问题选择预测的解剖先验，并以学习到的任务特定强度引导解码器注意力。将解剖定位预训练与CARA结合，得到CARA-VL模型。

Result: 实验表明CARA-VL在多种CMR成像设置下的临床评估与区域定位方面表现突出，并在外部临床队列上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该数据与方法为研究和推进VLM在心脏视觉理解方面提供了实用框架，相关问答数据将在发表后基于公开数据集公开。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (CMR) enables assessment of cardiac anatomy, ventricular function, and myocardial tissue characteristics. Clinicians interpret these images by identifying cardiac structures and focusing on the regions relevant to each clinical question, motivating anatomically guided vision-language models (VLMs). Yet CMR-specific supervision for anatomical localisation and clinical question answering remains limited. To address this gap, we investigate fine-grained CMR visual question answering through anatomical grounding and guided attention. We construct 128,915 anatomical-grounding and 42,799 clinical QA pairs across short-axis cine, late gadolinium enhancement, and long-axis cine. These datasets support anatomical recognition, localisation, and clinical assessment without requiring paired reports for individual training images. To help the model learn where to look, we introduce Cardiac Anatomy-Routed Attention (CARA), which selects predicted anatomical priors according to the question and guides decoder attention with learned task-specific strengths. Combining anatomical grounding pretraining with CARA yields our model, CARA-VL. Experiments demonstrate CARA-VL's strengths in clinical assessment and regional localisation across CMR imaging settings, with promising generalization to an external clinical cohort. Together, our data and method provide a practical framework for studying and advancing cardiac visual understanding in VLMs. We will release the QA data derived from public datasets upon publication.

</details>


### [161] [NavHarness: Adaptive Goals for Agentic Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39915)
*Haoxiang Shi,Zaijing Li,Muhe Ding,Xiang Deng,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Agentic VLN框架，通过Goal、Verify、Memory、Visuomotor四个智能体实现自适应目标设定、动态验证与多模态历史压缩，在R2R-CE、RxR-CE及真实世界评测中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航(VLN)中，通用多模态智能体虽能选择局部合理动作，但无法保证长程任务中执行与预期路线一致；且累积的交互历史不断增加后续决策的输入，带来显著推理开销。

Method: 框架包含四个智能体：(1)Goal Agent根据指令、当前观测和执行历史制定自适应目标；(2)Visuomotor Agent执行导航动作以实现每个目标；(3)Verify Agent通过目标特定的验证问题动态评估观测结果是否满足完成条件；(4)Memory Agent在验证目标完成后，以此边界压缩对应的多模态交互历史，同时保留后续导航所需信息。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上评估导航性能，在三种模型骨干上检验框架变体，并研究执行过程中的上下文演化。真实世界评测中，在八条挑战性路线上各测三次，达到83.3%成功率和1.51米导航误差。

Conclusion: 该Agentic VLN框架通过目标设定、动态验证与记忆压缩的协同，有效提升长程导航的一致性与效率，并在仿真与真实环境中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires embodied agents to generate actions based on instructions and observations. General-purpose multimodal agents offer a promising basis for this task, but selecting plausible local actions does not ensure that execution remains consistent with the intended route, particularly in long-horizon tasks. Moreover, the accumulated interaction history increases the input required for subsequent decisions, resulting in a significant inference overhead. To this end, we introduce \method, an Agentic VLN framework that includes a Goal Agent that sets adaptive goals for local actions, a Verify Agent that dynamically verifies whether a goal has been completed, a Memory Agent for multimodal context compression, and a Visuomotor Agent to execute adaptive goals. Specifically, the Goal Agent formulates adaptive goals based on the instruction, current observation, and execution history. Then the Visuomotor Agent executes navigation actions to achieve each goal, while the Verify Agent uses a goal-specific verification question to dynamically assess whether the observed outcomes satisfy the intended completion condition. Verified goal completion then marks a boundary for the Memory Agent to compress the corresponding multimodal interaction history while preserving information needed for subsequent navigation. We evaluate navigation on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, examine framework variants across three model backbones, and study context evolution during execution. For Real-World evaluation, \method achieves 83.3\% success and 1.51\,m navigation error across eight challenging routes evaluated three times each.

</details>


### [162] [CoVisco: Codec-Native Vision Encoder with Native Token Compression for Unified Image-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39924)
*Yulong Liu,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Guibo Zhu,Sirui Han,Dianhai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoVisco是一个原生支持令牌压缩的编解码视觉编码器，通过分段注意力与可学习抽象令牌，实现单次前向长视频编码，压缩视觉令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型面临缩放瓶颈：视觉令牌数量随时长和分辨率增长，使长视频理解成本高昂；现有方法多在密集编码后再压缩令牌，导致训练表示与部署所需紧凑接口不匹配。

Method: 结合编解码原生输入与分段注意力，单次前向编码长视觉输入；每段配备可学习抽象令牌学习段级紧凑表示，保留细粒度patch令牌；交替使用段内层与抽象通信层保持视频级上下文；轻量选择器输出纯抽象令牌或抽象令牌加运行时选取的patch令牌子集。

Result: 在565M图文对和6.4M视频上用对比目标预训练；视频嵌入与多模态理解基准表现有竞争力。在四段64帧设置下，仅抽象推理用400视觉令牌，性能接近甚至部分超OneVision-Encoder；选取patch令牌进一步提升细粒度视频推理。

Conclusion: CoVisco通过原生令牌压缩与编解码设计，为统一图像-视频理解提供紧凑高效视觉接口，降低下游MLLM视觉上下文与预填充负担，同时按需保留细粒度证据。

Abstract: Vision-language models face a fundamental scaling bottleneck: the number of visual tokens grows with both temporal duration and spatial resolution, making long-video understanding expensive for the vision encoder and the language model. Existing methods often compress visual tokens after dense encoding, creating a mismatch between the representation used during training and the compact interface required at deployment. We present CoVisco, a codec-native vision encoder with native token compression for unified image-video understanding. By combining codec-native input support with segmented attention, CoVisco can encode long visual inputs in a single forward pass without forming dense patch-to-patch interactions across all frames. Each temporal segment is equipped with learnable abstract tokens that learn a compact segment-level representation, while fine-grained patch tokens remain available throughout the encoder. Alternating intra-segment and abstract-communication layers preserve video-level context through the abstract-token channel. A lightweight selector further exposes either abstract tokens alone or abstract tokens augmented with a runtime-selected subset of patch tokens, yielding a compact visual interface that reduces the visual context and prefill burden of downstream MLLMs while retaining fine-grained evidence when needed. Pretrained with contrastive objectives on 565M image--text pairs and 6.4M videos, CoVisco shows competitive performance on video-oriented embedding and multimodal understanding benchmarks. In the evaluated four-segment, 64-frame setting, abstract-only inference uses only 400 visual tokens while achieving video-understanding performance close to, and on some benchmarks exceeding, OneVision-Encoder. Selected patch tokens further improve fine-grained video reasoning. Project URL: https://github.com/ernie-research/CoVisco.git

</details>


### [163] [Super-Resolving Unseen Hyperspectral Sensors at Any Scale via Spatial Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39926)
*Ji-Xuan He,Guohang Zhuang,Bo Junge,Tingyi Li,Lingchen,Miaomiao Cai,Yanan Qiao,Xiujin Liu,Junfeng Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniHSR，通过预测波段共享的空间算子（而非光谱值）实现跨传感器泛化和任意尺度的高光谱超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱超分辨率中，单一模型难以同时实现跨传感器泛化和任意尺度重建；现有方法在训练范围外的传感器或尺度上需要额外数据和计算才能保持重建质量。

Method: OmniHSR预测波段共享的空间算子而非光谱值。包含跨光谱映射（CSM）：将任意波段数的输入重采样到固定参考位置，并预测具有高斯支撑的局部算子；以及连续算子场重建（COFR）：将这些算子组合成连续场并应用于所有原始波段，实现任意尺度重建。

Result: 在全部七个数据集上算子预测优于直接光谱值预测。仅用ARAD训练、参数量0.538M，在六个未见数据集上无需目标域训练或适配即超越所有直接迁移基线。在×2到×48的十二个上采样因子下，Pavia U和Chikusei平均PSNR较最强基线提升0.55 dB，并超越从头训练或适配的基线，推理速度最高快36倍。

Conclusion: 通过预测波段共享空间算子，OmniHSR以极小参数量实现了强大的跨传感器泛化与任意尺度重建能力，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Achieving cross-sensor generalization and arbitrary-scale reconstruction with a single model remains challenging in hyperspectral super-resolution (HSR). Although recent methods support arbitrary-scale reconstruction, applying them to new sensors or scales beyond the training range often requires additional data and computation to maintain reconstruction quality. To address these challenges, we propose OmniHSR, which predicts band-shared spatial operators rather than spectral values. Cross-Spectral Mapping (CSM) resamples inputs with any number of bands to fixed reference positions and predicts local operators with Gaussian supports. Continuous Operator-Field Reconstruction (COFR) composes these operators into a continuous field and applies them to all original bands for arbitrary-scale reconstruction. Experiments demonstrate that operator prediction outperforms direct spectral-value prediction on all seven datasets. Trained solely on ARAD with only 0.538M parameters, OmniHSR outperforms all directly transferred baselines on six unseen datasets without target-domain training data or adaptation. Across twelve upsampling factors from $\times2$ to $\times48$, it improves average PSNR on Pavia U and Chikusei by 0.55 dB over the strongest baseline. It also surpasses baselines trained from scratch or adapted on the target sensor and achieves up to $36\times$ faster inference. Our code will be publicly released soon.

</details>


### [164] [Learning to Reason with Compressed Context: Ground-Truth-Free Adaptation of OmniLLMs via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39953)
*Jianghao Wang,Ke Meng,Jian Li,Chi Cheng,Longyu Qi,Liyin Liang,Yifeng Qian,Chunbo Lai,Yutian Lin,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAFD自蒸馏框架，让OmniLLM适应压缩上下文，在几乎全部条件下提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OmniLLM长多模态token序列部署成本高，token压缩虽降本但激进压缩会降精度；现有工作多聚焦压缩机制设计，忽视让底层语言模型适应压缩后上下文。

Method: CAFD为无需真值的自蒸馏框架：以同一多模态样本的全token视图作为特权信息，全上下文自教师沿学生on-policy轨迹对压缩上下文学生提供软目标监督，无需参考答案、推理过程或正确性奖励。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，跨5个音视频基准、5种压缩流水线、5种部署预算评估，125个条件中120个提升，平均精度+1.44分，平均恢复26.9%精度差距。

Conclusion: 无需真值的适应方法为改善部署OmniLLM的精度-效率权衡提供了有效且实用的途径。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (OmniLLMs) enable unified audio-video understanding, but their long multimodal token sequences make deployment computationally expensive. Token compression reduces this cost, yet aggressive compression often lowers accuracy. Existing works predominantly focus on designing better compression mechanisms; however, adapting the underlying language model to reason effectively over the remaining compressed context remains under-explored. To address this, we propose CAFD (Compressed-Context Adaptation via Full-Context Distillation), a ground-truth-free self-distillation framework that adapts OmniLLMs to fixed compression pipelines without requiring reference answers, rationales, or correctness rewards. CAFD leverages the full-token view of the same multimodal sample as a source of privileged information: a full-context self-teacher provides soft target supervision to a compressed-context student along the student's on-policy trajectory. Evaluated on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B across five audio-video benchmarks, five compression pipelines, and five deployment budgets, CAFD demonstrates consistent gains, improving 120 out of 125 conditions with an average accuracy boost of 1.44 points and recovering 26.9% of the accuracy gap on average. These results demonstrate that the proposed ground-truth-free adaptation offers an effective and practical route to improving the accuracy-efficiency trade-off in deployed OmniLLMs.

</details>


### [165] [Reconstructing the Dynamic World: A Representation-Centric View of 4D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39960)
*Ziren Gong,Guo Chen,Yongjia Li,Yihua Shao,Fabio Tosi,Stefano Mattoccia,Matteo Poggi,Hao Tang,Fei Ma,Shuyan Li,Ziyang Yan,Nicu Sebe,Ling Shao,Jianfei Cai,Qi Tian,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是关于4D场景重建的统一综述，从场景表示、时序建模、重建流程和优化目标四个维度组织现有方法，并整合数据集与评价指标、指出当前实验局限与未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D场景重建需从视觉观测恢复动态环境不断演化的几何、外观与运动，但非刚性运动、遮挡、时序不一致以及重建保真度与计算效率的权衡使其依然困难。NeRF与3DGS虽带来多种动态场景表示与重建方案，但方法间关系、设计选择与评价协议仍较为分散，缺乏统一理解框架。

Method: 提出统一的4D场景重建视角，围绕场景表示、时序建模策略、重建流程与优化目标对现有方法进行归纳；在该框架下分析不同设计选择对几何保真度、外观一致性、运动表示与计算效率的影响；并梳理常用数据集与评价指标。

Result: 形成结构化的方法分类与比较框架，明确了各类设计选择与重建质量、效率等指标之间的关联，同时汇总了常用数据集与评价指标，并识别出当前实验实践中的局限。

Conclusion: 通过将方法学发展与底层假设、评价证据相连接，该工作为理解现有4D场景重建方法并识别未来研究方向提供了结构化基础，并维护了一个持续更新的相关论文与资源库。

Abstract: 4D scene reconstruction aims to recover the evolving geometry, appearance, and motion of dynamic environments from visual observations. Despite substantial progress in neural scene representations, reconstructing dynamic scenes remains challenging due to non-rigid motion, occlusions, temporal inconsistencies, and the trade-offs between reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency. Recent advances in Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have introduced diverse approaches to representing and reconstructing dynamic scenes, yet their relationships, underlying design choices, and evaluation protocols remain fragmented. In this paper, we present a unified perspective on 4D scene reconstruction, organizing existing methods around their scene representations, temporal modeling strategies, reconstruction pipelines, and optimization objectives. Through this framework, we examine how different design choices affect geometric fidelity, appearance consistency, motion representation, and computational efficiency. We further consolidate commonly used datasets and evaluation metrics, identify limitations in current experimental practices, and discuss open challenges in reconstructing complex, dynamic real-world environments. By connecting methodological developments with their underlying assumptions and evaluation evidence, this work provides a structured foundation for understanding existing approaches and identifying future research directions. An evolving collection of relevant papers and resources is available at https://github.com/ZiyangYan/Awesome-4D-Scene-Reconstruction.

</details>


### [166] [Can We Anticipate Violence? Multimodal Learning from Pre-Incident Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40014)
*Sindhuja Penchala,Mohammed Yusuf Mujawar,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究暴力事件发生前的短时风险识别，构建基于XD-Violence的正常-风险二分类数据集，融合面部、音频与动作多模态特征，DeiT-Tiny配置取得91.21%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴力发生后的检测已有较多研究，但更关键的是在事件发生前依据即时行为线索进行预警。本文旨在探索短时窗内、事件前的风险识别，以提前发现潜在暴力风险。

Method: 从带时间标注的XD-Violence片段构建Normal-versus-Risky二分类任务，使用443个样本并做源级划分。每个样本为变长的事件前片段（时长由可观测行为上下文决定，且排除事件本身）。评估三类互补信息源：面部区域外观、时间对齐音频、基于关键点跟踪的身体运动特征，并用Swin-Tiny、ViT-Tiny、DeiT-Tiny在相同划分下进行受控消融实验。

Result: 所有模态组合优于任何单一或部分组合。最佳配置为DeiT-Tiny结合音频、面部外观与动作，在留出测试集上达到91.21%准确率、88.96%平衡准确率、93.65% F1和96.38% ROC-AUC。

Conclusion: 外观、声学与运动学线索具有互补性，可为识别事件前升高的风险提供有效证据，支持短时窗事前风险预警的可行性。

Abstract: Detecting violence after it begins is important from recognizing behavioral cues that appear immediately beforehand. This work studies short-horizon pre-incident risk recognition from multimodal video signals. We construct a binary Normal-versus-Risky setting from temporally annotated XD-Violence clips, using 443 samples with source-level separation across training, validation, and test sets. Each sample consists of a variable-length pre-incident clip, with its duration determined by the observable behavioral context preceding the incident. The inci- dent itself is excluded from all input clips. We evaluate three complementary information sources: facial-region appearance, temporally aligned audio, and body-motion features derived from tracked keypoints. Controlled ablations are performed with Swin-Tiny, ViT-Tiny, and DeiT-Tiny to measure the contribution of each modality under the same split. Results show that combining all modalities is more effective than using any other combination alone. The best configuration, Deit-Tiny with audio, facial appearance, and motion, achieves 91.21% accuracy, 88.96% balanced accuracy, 93.65% F1-score, and 96.38% ROC-AUC on the held-out test set. These results suggest that complementary appearance, acoustic, and kinematic cues provide useful evidence for recognizing elevated pre-incident risk.

</details>


### [167] [WARP: A Unified Benchmark for Invisible Image Watermarking -- Robustness and Protection Against Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40031)
*Khaled Abud,Aleksey Yakushev,Aleksandr Akimenkov,Irina Serzhenko,Kirill Aistov,Egor Kovalev,Dmitry Obydenkov,Sergey Lavrushkin,Anastasia Antsiferova,Dmitriy Vatolin,Yury Markin,Kirill Lukianov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WARP，一个评估隐形水印鲁棒性的统一框架与基准，涵盖32种水印方法和34种擦除攻击，并给出当前最鲁棒方法的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着法规与行业实践要求对AI生成内容进行标记并保证来源可追溯，数字图像隐形水印日益重要；而现有基准测试方法已无法反映最新的水印技术与评估标准，亟需更新。

Method: 提出WARP统一框架与基准：集成32种近期经典、深度与生成式水印方法，以及34种擦除技术（从传统失真到对抗、净化和重嵌入攻击），并提供标准化、可复现、易扩展的协议，用于评估感知质量、水印可读性和抗攻击能力。

Result: 利用WARP对当前隐形水印技术进行广泛评估，构建了该领域规模最大的鲁棒性基准；识别出在失真与对抗条件下最鲁棒的方法，揭示水印方法与最有效攻击策略之间的稳定对应关系，并发现部分方法虽对标准失真鲁棒，却对重嵌入攻击极为脆弱。

Conclusion: WARP为隐形水印鲁棒性评估提供了统一、可扩展的基准平台，代码已开源，有助于推动水印技术的标准化评测与后续研究。

Abstract: Digital image watermarking is increasingly critical in media contexts, as emerging regulations and industry practices require marking AI-generated content and ensuring traceable sources to prevent manipulation or misuse. Recent advances in invisible watermarking methods highlight the need to update existing benchmarking practices to reflect current techniques and evaluation criteria.
  We address this by introducing WARP -- a unified framework and benchmark for evaluating the robustness of invisible watermarks. WARP incorporates 32 recent classical, deep, and generative watermarking methods, as well as 34 different erasing techniques, ranging from traditional distortions to more sophisticated adversarial, purification, and re-embedding attacks. It provides standardized, reproducible, and easily scalable protocols for evaluating perceptual quality, watermark readability, and attack resilience.
  Using WARP, we extensively evaluate current invisible watermarking techniques, collecting the largest robustness benchmark in the field. Results identify the most robust approaches under both distortion and adversarial conditions, and reveal consistent relationships between watermarking methods and the attack strategies most effective against them. Our experiments also highlight that some of the watermarking methods considered are highly vulnerable to reembedding, even if they are robust to standard distortions. The code is made available at https://github.com/ispras/wibe.

</details>


### [168] [Enhancing Autoregressive Video Generation via Representation Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40037)
*Fangyu Lin,Xingtong Ge,Lunjie Zhu,Yi Zhang,Zhening Liu,Tianhang Wang,Mengfei Li,Yumeng Zhang,Guanglu Song,Yu Liu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Radian，一种表征空间对抗蒸馏框架，在冻结视觉基础模型的特征空间中用真实数据对抗监督补充 on-policy DMD，实现少步自回归视频生成，训练后额外组件全部丢弃，不改变推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频生成虽能高效流式合成，但早期时序块的误差会作为上下文被复用并沿后续 rollout 传播，导致细节退化、结构漂移与运动不稳定。现有 DMD 主要在扩散潜空间中对齐学生与教师分布，对解码后视频的感知质量缺乏直接监督。

Method: Radian 在冻结 VFM 定义的特征空间中引入真实数据对抗监督：训练时稀疏解码自回归学生 rollout 的帧，提取多层视觉表征，用轻量判别头区分生成输出与真实视频帧。DMD 目标将学生锚定到预训练教师，表征空间对抗目标提供互补的感知与语义梯度以促进高质量模态。这些额外组件训练后丢弃，生成器架构与推理时去噪预算保持不变。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-1.3B 上覆盖四步分块、一步逐帧与分钟级自回归生成。四步生成下取得 VBench Total 0.8444、VideoAlign Total 0.8033；相比 Rolling Forcing 将 VBench-Long 从 0.7805 提升至 0.8041，且去噪步数更少。图像、视频、扩散表征的受控对比表明不同表征空间诱导出不同对抗信号，外部 VFM 梯度比扩散内部特征的对抗监督更能有效补充 DMD。

Conclusion: 在表征空间引入基于真实数据的对抗监督可有效弥补 DMD 缺失的感知监督，缓解自回归误差传播，在多种少步生成设置下提升视频质量与长视频稳定性，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generation enables efficient streaming synthesis, but errors introduced in early temporal blocks are reused as context and can propagate through subsequent rollouts, leading to detail degradation, structural drift, and unstable motion. Existing distribution matching distillation (DMD) primarily aligns student and teacher distributions in diffusion latent space, but provides no direct supervision over the perceptual quality of decoded videos. We introduce Radian, a representation-space adversarial distillation framework that complements on-policy DMD with real-data adversarial supervision in the feature space defined by a frozen visual foundation model (VFM). During training, Radian sparsely decodes frames from autoregressive student rollouts, extracts multi-level visual representations, and applies lightweight discriminator heads to distinguish generated outputs from real video frames. The DMD objective anchors the student to the pretrained teacher, while the representation-space adversarial objective supplies complementary perceptual and semantic gradients that promote high-quality modes. These additional components are discarded after training, leaving the generator architecture and inference-time denoising budget unchanged. Experiments on Wan2.1-1.3B cover four-step chunk-wise, one-step frame-wise, and minute-long autoregressive generation. Our method achieves a VBench Total of 0.8444 and a VideoAlign Total of 0.8033 under four-step generation, and improves VBench-Long from 0.7805 to 0.8041 over Rolling Forcing while using fewer denoising steps. Controlled comparisons across image, video, and diffusion representations further indicate that the choice of representation spaces induces distinct adversarial signals, and external VFM gradients complement DMD more effectively than adversarial supervision derived from diffusion-internal features.

</details>


### [169] [CoEvoWhen: Policy-Tool Coevolution for Ultra-Long Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40048)
*Yiduo Jia,Muzhi Zhu,Jinchuan Shi,Hao Zhong,Yuling Xi,Ke Liu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种策略-工具协同进化框架，让VLM在不更新模型参数的情况下，通过从自身推理轨迹中联合进化高层策略与可执行媒体工具，形成可复用技能，从而提升超长视频时序定位的精度并降低视觉token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长视频时序定位需在有限视觉预算下兼顾长程证据搜索与细粒度事件理解，但现有智能体方法大多依赖预定义策略与固定工具能力，缺乏自适应性与可迁移性。

Method: 提出策略-工具协同进化框架：由外部技能更新器从VLM的智能体推理轨迹中蒸馏可迁移的长视频时序定位任务经验，据此优化长程图像观测与细粒度视频观测的编排策略；同时利用其编码能力升级现有工具或创建新工具以适配长视频证据获取。进化后的技能使VLM自主编排工具、协调图像与视频观测进行推理，无需依赖额外的更强规划模型。

Result: 在五个基准、三种VLM上的大量实验表明，该协同进化方法持续提升超长视频时序定位精度，同时降低推理时的视觉token成本；且进化出的技能在无需额外任务特定进化的情况下，在通用长视频问答上也取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 策略-工具协同进化框架有效且具有泛化性，可形成免参数更新的可复用技能，为长视频理解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Ultra-long video temporal grounding requires balancing long-range evidence search with fine-grained event understanding under a limited visual budget, yet existing agentic methods still rely largely on predefined policies and tool capabilities. Motivated by this, we propose a novel policy-tool coevolution framework that jointly evolves high-level policies and executable media tools from the agentic reasoning trajectories of a VLM, forming a reusable skill without updating model parameters. During evolution, an external skill updater distills transferable task experience in long-video temporal grounding, accordingly refining the orchestration of long-range image-based and fine-grained video-based observations. Alongside these policy updates, the updater employs its coding capabilities to upgrade existing tools or create new ones, adapting the tools to long-video evidence acquisition. Equipped with the evolved skill, the VLM autonomously orchestrates tools under the guidance of the evolved policy, coordinating image and video observations for agentic inference without relying on a separate, stronger planning model. Extensive experiments spanning five benchmarks and three VLMs show that policy-tool coevolution consistently improves temporal grounding accuracy in ultra-long videos while reducing visual token cost at inference, and that the evolved skill yields substantial performance gains on general long-video QA without additional task-specific evolution, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of our framework for long-video understanding.

</details>


### [170] [Less Data, Better Timing: Student-Curriculum Coupling for VLM On-Policy Distillation in Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40055)
*Jiacheng Qiu,Yunsoo Kim,Ruichen Xu,Jian Luo,Petar M. Djurić,Sima Mofakham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出学生-课程耦合框架SCC，通过闭环课程设计实现视频时序定位的高效后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线蒸馏(OPD)流程从固定教师和初始学生状态构建训练课程，隐含假设所选样本在整个优化过程中始终保持正向监督价值；但监督可信性与监督必要性是两个不同却耦合的概念，后者随学生当前任务能力而变化。

Method: 提出学生-课程耦合(SCC)闭环框架：由紧凑的Anchor-Frontier课程定义候选监督空间，进化的学生动态决定激活子集，使监督可随能力变化被激活、暂停或重新激活，将教师计算和优化集中于当前任务层面的不足。

Result: 在三个TVG基准上，SCC相较Video-OPD在原课程上平均召回率相对提升5.1%，同时训练样本减少60.0%、训练时间减少50.4%；消融实验支持能力结构化课程设计与学生依赖监督的互补作用。

Conclusion: SCC是一个数据与计算高效的TVG后训练框架，通过将可信监督与学生学习需求对齐，实现更强的时序定位性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense supervision directly on student-generated trajectories, making it an effective post-training strategy for vision-language models in temporal video grounding (TVG). However, existing pipelines typically construct the training curriculum from a fixed teacher and the initial student state, implicitly assuming that selected examples retain positive supervision value throughout optimization. We show that supervision trustworthiness and supervision necessity are distinct yet coupled: the former concerns target credibility, while the latter varies with the student's current task competence; together, they shape supervision value. Building on this coupled view, we introduce Student-Curriculum Coupling (SCC), a closed-loop framework in which a compact Anchor-Frontier curriculum defines the candidate supervision space and the evolving student dynamically determines its active subset. Supervision can therefore be activated, suspended, or reactivated as competence changes, concentrating teacher computation and optimization on current task-level deficits. Across three TVG benchmarks, SCC achieves a 5.1% relative improvement in mean recall over Video-OPD on its original curriculum, while using 60.0% fewer training examples and reducing training time by 50.4%. Ablations support the complementary roles of capability-structured curriculum design and student-dependent supervision in achieving these gains. Together, these results establish SCC as a data- and compute-efficient framework for TVG post-training, delivering stronger temporal grounding by aligning trustworthy supervision with the student's evolving learning needs.

</details>


### [171] [LongEmo: Towards Emotion Understanding and Reasoning in Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40079)
*Shuo Zhang,Yifan Zhou,Han Wang,Jinsong Zhang,Jingyu Li,Hongbing Li,Zhejun Zhang,Chengyi Zhao,Yuquan Hao,Yitong Liu,Jiyin Li,Ruiqi Tang,Zixuan Lin,Yi Luo,Xurui Zhang,Ronghao Chen,Huacan Wang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出长视频情感理解与推理基准 LongEmoBench，以及记忆增强智能体框架 LongEmo，实现从连续场景交互到复杂情节发展的渐进式情感推理，并取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在情感计算上多局限于短视频片段和有限交互，而真实情感是受过往经验与进行中事件共同塑造的动态累积过程，因此需要长视频情感理解与推理的评测与模型。

Method: 提出 LongEmoBench 基准，评测从连续场景交互到复杂情节发展的渐进能力；提出 LongEmo 记忆增强智能体框架，处理连续视频流构建事件记忆图，显式建模长程依赖与跨离散事件的情感动态，并根据问题检索相关事件流、迭代整合多模态记忆与关系依赖以推断答案。

Result: 对 17 种代表性方法的广泛评测显示，它们在长视频情感理解与推理上表现明显不足；LongEmo 取得最优性能，验证了以事件为中心的记忆架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 长视频情感推理需要显式的事件级记忆建模，LongEmo 的事件记忆图架构有效弥补现有模型在长程情感推理上的不足。

Abstract: While recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promise in affective computing, their reasoning capabilities are largely confined to short video clips with limited interactions. However, real-world emotions are not merely isolated instantaneous reactions but dynamic and cumulative processes deeply shaped by past experiences and ongoing events. To bridge this gap, we introduce LongEmoBench, a benchmark dedicated to emotion understanding and reasoning in long videos. It assesses progressive capabilities scaling from continuous scene interactions to complex episodic developments. Furthermore, we propose LongEmo, a novel memory-augmented agentic framework designed to tackle the immense challenges of long-range affective reasoning. LongEmo processes continuous video streams to construct an Event Memory Graph, explicitly modeling long-range dependencies and capturing emotional dynamics across discrete events. Given a question, the agent retrieves a query-relevant event stream from the graph, iteratively integrating multimodal memories and relational dependencies to deduce the final answer. Extensive evaluations of 17 representative methods reveal that they struggle significantly with emotion understanding and reasoning in long videos. In contrast, LongEmo achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the efficacy of its event-centric memory architecture.

</details>


### [172] [GateSPINE: Gated Cross-View Fusion for Lumbar Spine MRI Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40091)
*Hoang Nguyen Van,Cuong Vuong Tuan,Trang Mai Xuan,Bien Tran Van,Nam Tran Van,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GateSPINE，一个视觉-语言框架，用于多序列、多平面腰椎MRI的自动报告生成，通过门控跨视图融合提升临床效能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI报告生成方法将检查编码为单一体积，并用固定规则组合多次采集，导致仅在某一平面可见的发现被稀释和遗漏，降低了临床效能指标的召回率，而遗漏异常代价最高。

Method: GateSPINE将矢状位T1与T2体积用免训练算子融合，用两个并行3D编码器分别编码融合后的矢状位与轴位体积，再解码为报告；核心是门控跨视图融合模块，按特征通道和token预测各视图的接纳程度，使信息量更大的视图在相应空间位置占主导。

Result: 在三个腰椎MRI数据集（两个公开基准和一个来自Phenikaa大学医院的私有队列）上，GateSPINE在NLG与CE指标下均取得最高的CE F1，并在三个数据集上通过提升召回率实现；在缺少轴位序列的SPIDER上，提升来自矢状位融合组件而非门控跨视图机制（后者在含两个成像平面的两个队列上得到验证）；标准NLG指标上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 门控跨视图融合能有效利用多平面MRI的互补信息，减少遗漏，提升临床效能召回率与F1，同时保持自然语言生成质量。

Abstract: Automated report generation can ease the burden radiolo gists face when interpreting multi-sequence MRI studies. Unlike CT, MRI examinations comprise multiple sequences and imaging planes, each con tributing complementary diagnostic information. Existing methods en code a study as a single volume and combine multiple acquisitions by fixed rules. Findings visible in only one plane are thus diluted and of ten missed, lowering recall on clinical efficacy metrics, where a missed abnormality is most costly. We propose GateSPINE, a vision-language framework that fuses sagittal T1 and T2 volumes with a training-free operator, encodes the fused sagittal and axial volumes with two parallel 3D encoders, and decodes their combined representation into a report. Its core mechanism is a gated cross view fusion module that predicts, per feature channel and token, how much of each view to admit, so the more informative view dominates at each spatial location. We evaluate GateSPINE on three lumbar MRI datasets, comprising two public bench marks and a private cohort collected from Phenikaa University Hospital, using both natural language generation (NLG) and clinical efficacy (CE) metrics. GateSPINE achieves the highest CE F1 through improved re call on all three datasets; on SPIDER, which lacks an axial sequence, this reflects the sagittal fusion component rather than the gated cross-view mechanism, which is validated on the two cohorts with both imaging planes. GateSPINE also remains competitive on standard NLG metrics.

</details>


### [173] [VR-JEPA: Learning Contrastive-State Latent Guidance for Generation-based Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40129)
*Zehua Ma,Kun Xiang,Yunshuang Nie,Quanlin Chen,Haoyuan Li,Xiuwei Chen,Jiang Ji,Haijun Wu,Zhenyu Xie,Michael Kampffmeyer,Hanhui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VR-JEPA 框架，通过对齐 V-JEPA 预测器与任务特定推理逻辑，并用其预测的潜在轨迹引导视频生成，从而提升视觉推理的可靠性与逻辑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型缺乏对潜在视觉状态如何演化的显式指导，生成轨迹容易出现物理和结构上的不一致，削弱推理可靠性；而 V-JEPA 虽提供丰富的时空先验，却无法自然适配复杂视觉任务所需的逻辑推理能力。因此需要弥合这一差距。

Method: VR-JEPA 包含两个核心设计：(i) 在相同输入条件下，将成功轨迹与生成的替代轨迹配对，利用其 V-JEPA 表征差异识别有信息量的状态和 token，进行局部对比监督；(ii) 为 V-JEPA 预测器配备在锚任务数据上训练的技能特定专家，使其能跨认知领域自适应地专化共享时空先验。结合对比监督，模型可预测为视频生成提供任务特定逻辑指导的潜在轨迹。

Result: 在大规模 VBVR-Pro-Bench 数据集上，VR-JEPA 相比最先进的基于生成的推理基线取得 11.33% 的相对提升，显著减轻物理伪影并增强逻辑一致性。

Conclusion: 通过将 V-JEPA 预测器与任务特定推理逻辑对齐并引导视频生成，VR-JEPA 有效提升了生成轨迹的物理合理性与逻辑一致性，为基于视频生成的视觉推理提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Reasoning through video generation offers a promising path toward visual intelligence by modeling latent visual states and their dynamics. However, current video generation models often lack explicit guidance on how these states should evolve, leaving generated trajectories prone to physical and structural inconsistencies that undermine reasoning reliability. While the Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) provides rich spatiotemporal priors learned through latent prediction, these general priors do not naturally adapt to the logical reasoning capabilities required for complex visual tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose VR-JEPA, a framework that aligns the V-JEPA predictor with task-specific reasoning logic through localized contrastive-state learning and uses its predicted latent trajectories to guide video generation for visual reasoning. Specifically, (i) we pair successful trajectories with generated alternatives under the same input conditions and use discrepancies in their V-JEPA representations to identify informative states and tokens for localized contrastive supervision. (ii) We further equip the V-JEPA predictor with skill-specific experts trained on anchor-task data, allowing the model to adaptively specialize its shared spatiotemporal priors across diverse cognitive domains. Together with skill-specific experts, this contrastive supervision enables VR-JEPA to predict latent trajectories that provide task-specific logical guidance for video generation. Comprehensive experiments on the large-scale VBVR-Pro-Bench dataset demonstrate that VR-JEPA achieves an $11.33\%$ relative improvement over the cutting-edge generation-based reasoning baseline, significantly mitigating physical artifacts and enhancing logical consistency.

</details>


### [174] [Recognition of Urbanized Areas in UAV-Derived Very-High-Resolution Visible-Light Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40212)
*Edyta Puniach,Wojciech Gruszczyński,Paweł Ćwiąkała,Katarzyna Strząbała,Elżbieta Pastucha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对比基于无人机RGB影像的城市化/非城市化区域分类方法，神经网络（约96%）优于最佳植被指数Excess Blue（约87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同分类器在无人机RGB影像上区分城市化与非城市化区域的效果，并考察季节对植被指数阈值、以及影像块尺寸对神经网络精度的影响。

Method: 在两个研究区（GSD分别为10 mm与15 mm）开展调查，测试多种植被指数阈值法与神经网络；人工制作参考分类（分别约2400万与380万像素）；分析季节对阈值的影响及输入影像块尺寸对NN精度的影响。

Result: 神经网络分类精度更高（约96%），优于最佳植被指数Excess Blue（约87%）；由于数据集高度不平衡（非城市化区域约占87%），同时采用Matthews相关系数评估。

Conclusion: 神经网络在城市/非城市区域分类中表现优于植被指数阈值法；结合统计指标与定性评估，识别出两类方法分类差异的主要来源。

Abstract: This study compared classifiers that differentiate between urbanized and non-urbanized areas based on unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-acquired RGB imagery. The tested solutions in-cluded numerous vegetation indices (VIs) thresholding and neural networks (NNs). The analysis was conducted for two study areas for which surveys were carried out using different UAVs and cameras. The ground sampling distances for the study areas were 10 mm and 15 mm, respectively. Reference classification was performed manually, obtaining approximately 24 million classified pix-els for the first area and approximately 3.8 million for the second. This research study included an analysis of the impact of the season on the threshold values for the tested VIs and the impact of image patch size provided as inputs for the NNs on classification accuracy. The results of the con-ducted research study indicate a higher classification accuracy using NNs (about 96%) compared with the best of the tested VIs, i.e., Excess Blue (about 87%). Due to the highly imbalanced nature of the used datasets (non-urbanized areas constitute approximately 87% of the total datasets), the Mat-thews correlation coefficient was also used to assess the correctness of the classification. The analysis based on statistical measures was supplemented with a qualitative assessment of the classification results, which allowed the identification of the most important sources of differences in classification between VIs thresholding and NNs.

</details>


### [175] [Learning Skills from Historical Action Trajectories: Action Experience Dictionary for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40219)
*Qi Lyu,Jiahua Dong,Hao Shen,Xudong Wang,Hongyuan Yu,Baichen Liu,Henghui Ding,Zhi Han,Nicu Sebe,Ivan Laptev,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV
