Moreover, restricting the editor to causal attention lowers acceptance, especially on highly predictable outputs, indicating that future context is a key source of these gains.

</details>


### [209] [Shifting Mechanisms: How Positional Encoding Choice Shapes In-Context Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38530)
*Eric Enouen,Sainyam Galhotra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文从机制角度研究位置编码（PE）选择如何塑造语言模型的上下文检索机制。通过对22个开源模型和受控预训练消融实验的分析，发现标准RoPE模型主要依赖位置检索，而PE混合模型转向语义检索，并揭示其长上下文增益掩盖了检索权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型越来越多地在不同层使用不同的注意力跨度和位置编码（如RoPE配合滑窗注意力、NoPE配合全局注意力，即SWA NoPE），但这些选择如何影响上下文检索尚不清楚，因此需要从机制视角进行系统研究。

Method: 采用机制可解释性视角，追踪PE选择如何塑造模型用于上下文检索的内部机制；分析跨越8个家族、22个开源权重模型；并进行受控预训练消融实验，检验将位置编码限制在局部层的影响。

Result: 标准RoPE模型主要依赖位置检索，而PE混合模型转向语义检索；受控预训练消融表明，将位置编码限制在局部层会产生这种语义转变，并削弱位置信息表征；SWA NoPE在多目标检索和QA上优于RoPE，但在区分竞争性键时性能下降。

Conclusion: PE混合模型所报告的长上下文增益掩盖了检索权衡，这些行为差异更能反映从位置机制向语义机制的转变，而非长上下文检索的统一改进。

Abstract: Language models increasingly use architectures that vary attention span and positional encoding across layers, such as applying RoPE with sliding-window attention and NoPE with global attention (SWA NoPE). However, how these choices shape in-context retrieval remains unclear. To study this question, we take a mechanistic view, tracing how positional encoding (PE) choice shapes the internal mechanisms models use for in-context retrieval. Across 22 open-weight models spanning eight families, we find that standard RoPE models rely primarily on positional retrieval, while PE hybrids shift toward semantic retrieval. We further show on a controlled pre-training ablation that confining positional encoding to local layers produces this semantic shift, degrading representations of positional information. Finally, we show that the reported long-context gains of PE hybrids mask a retrieval trade-off: SWA NoPE improves over RoPE on multiple-target retrieval and QA, but degrades when distinguishing competing keys. We show that these behavioral differences better track the mechanism shift from positional toward semantic mechanisms than a uniform improvement in long-context retrieval.

</details>


### [210] [MedKIT: Evaluating Knowledge Integration and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38543)
*Lukas Thede,Yash Kumar Atri,David Chen,Danielle Bitterman,Matthias Bethge,Tom Hartvigsen,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MedKIT 基准，用于细粒度评估模型在真实临床知识更新序列下对知识的整合与迁移能力，并发现"可回忆"与"可使用"之间存在持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界知识不断演变，模型需持续更新；在医学领域，临床证据随时间变化，过时知识会带来安全风险。现有知识整合评测多聚焦事实回忆，难以判断新整合的知识是否真正可用。

Method: 构建基准 MedKIT，每个实例对应一个由临床证据推导的事实更新，并配备针对性探针，评估词汇变化、关系变换、组合推理和开放式应用等迁移能力，同时用局部性测试检验知识保持。基于该基准，对 5 个模型（含通用与医学 LLM）上的 12 种知识整合策略开展大规模实证研究。

Result: 结果显示出回忆与可用知识之间的持续差距：多数方法在原始更新任务和词汇变化下提升明显，但关系泛化有限，且在组合推理或开放式应用任务上没有任何方法带来有意义的提升。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了知识整合中的根本性挑战，并将 MedKIT 定位为开发新方法的测试平台，以推动新整合知识在跨任务、跨情境下更一致地可用。

Abstract: Constantly evolving real-world knowledge necessitates models to be updated continuously. Especially in medicine, as clinical evidence changes over time, outdated knowledge can pose safety risks. Existing evaluations of knowledge integration focus on factual recall, offering limited insight into whether newly integrated knowledge is actually usable. Our benchmark MedKIT (Medical Knowledge Integration and Transfer) provides a granular evaluation of how models integrate and apply knowledge under realistic sequences of clinical updates. Each instance corresponds to a factual update derived from clinical evidence, paired with targeted probes that assess transfer across lexical variation, relational transformations, compositional reasoning, and open-ended operationalization, as well as locality tests for knowledge preservation. Using MedKIT, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of 12 knowledge integration strategies across 5 diverse models, including both general-purpose and medical LLMs. Our results reveal a consistent gap between recall and usable knowledge: while most methods achieve strong gains on the original update task and under lexical variation, relational generalization is limited, and no method yields meaningful improvements on compositional or operational tasks. These findings highlight a fundamental challenge in knowledge integration and position MedKIT as a testbed for developing methods that make newly integrated knowledge more consistently usable across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [211] [Prompt2Skill: Unsupervised Skill Optimization From Natural Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38593)
*Bo Ni,Li Li,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prompt2Skill 框架，仅凭自然语言任务描述即可自动构建技能，通过推导任务规范、发现或合成数据集并进行闭环反思式编辑，在四个领域上平均提升 10.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家编写的技能成本高昂，且未针对具体消费它们的模型进行优化，其失效模式会随模型版本、规模和训练而变化；新兴任务可能超出已有技能库范围，需在训练数据可用前开发新技能；已有自动化技能优化方法依赖人工整理的分布内训练集，而用户未必具备。

Method: Prompt2Skill：从自然语言任务描述出发，推导任务规范，发现或合成数据集，并在闭环的反思式编辑中精炼技能。

Result: 在问答、阅读理解、电子表格操作和数学推理四个领域，Prompt2Skill 持续优于直接提示基线，在开源与前沿模型上平均提升 10.8。

Conclusion: 仅依靠任务描述即可自动构建并优化技能，无需人工整理的训练数据，能够有效提升 LLM 在专业领域的表现。

Abstract: Skills are external artifacts that Large Language Models (LLMs) consume at inference time to improve their performance on specialized domains by incorporating relevant procedural and domain knowledge. Expert-authored skills are expensive to produce, and the resulting artifacts are not optimized for the specific model that consumes them, whose failure modes can vary with version, scale and training. In addition, emerging tasks may fall outside the scope of existing skill libraries, creating a need to develop new skills before curated training data become available. Recent works have explored automated skill optimization through reflection, but they require a curated, in-distribution training set, which users might not always have. To address these limitations, we present Prompt2Skill, a framework that builds skills from natural-language task description alone. From the prompt, the system derives a task specification, discovers or synthesizes datasets, and refines the skill in a closed loop of reflective editing. Across four domains spanning question answering, reading comprehension, spreadsheet manipulation, and mathematical reasoning, Prompt2Skill consistently outperforms the direct prompting baseline, achieving an average improvement of 10.8 across open-source and frontier models.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond Oracle Communication: Benchmarking Interactive Intent Alignment Under Miscommunication and Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38604)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Mengyuan Chao,Ke Xiao,Ziyi Chen,Daoan Zhang,Yan Zhang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"交互式意图对齐"任务设定，并构建了可执行基准 Drift-Bench++ 及评测协议 GRIP，用于评估 LLM 智能体在不完美沟通与意图演变下持续追踪用户真实意图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准普遍假设用户总能准确、充分地表达固定意图（oracle communication 假设），而现实中用户会误传信息、改变目标、失去耐心，这一假设很少成立，因此需要新设定与基准来研究真实交互条件下的意图对齐。

Method: 提出 Drift-Bench++：一套原则化的基准构建流程，用于生成经验证的可执行任务，并带有受控的沟通错位与意图偏移；交互协议包含有限耐心、多样化模拟用户和静默的、受交互条件影响的意图偏移。同时提出 GRIP 评测协议，覆盖任务落地、用户真实性、询问有效性和对演变意图的适应。

Result: 在多种环境、模型和交互条件下，更强的交互一致性地带来帮助，但仍远未达到 oracle 性能；在已部署的 ProdAgent 会话上的验证表明，所建模的失败模式在实际部署中普遍且影响重大。

Conclusion: Drift-Bench++ 为交互式意图对齐提供了统一、可执行的基准，为在真实沟通与意图演变条件下评估和推进智能体研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly tackle complex tasks through interactive, long-horizon exchanges with users, while existing benchmarks generally assume that users always accurately and sufficiently communicate a fixed intent. However, this oracle communication assumption rarely holds in practice: users may miscommunicate, change their goals, and run out of patience. We define this task setting as Interactive Intent Alignment, where agents must recover and continuously track the user's current intent despite imperfect communication and evolving goals. To study this setting, we introduce Drift-Bench++, a principled benchmark construction pipeline for verified executable tasks with controlled misalignment and intent shifts, along with an interaction protocol featuring finite patience, diverse simulated users, and silent interaction-conditioned shifts. We further develop GRIP, a comprehensive evaluation protocol covering task grounding, user realism, inquiry effectiveness, and adaptation to evolving intent. Across diverse environments, models, and interaction conditions, stronger interaction consistently helps but remains far from oracle performance; Validation on deployed ProdAgent sessions further shows that the modeled failures are prevalent and consequential in deployment. By providing a unified, executable benchmark for interactive intent alignment, Drift-Bench++ offers a foundation for evaluating and advancing agents under realistic communication and evolving intent.

</details>


### [213] [StreamDecisionBench: Evaluating Decisions in Force on Evolving Language Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38612)
*Jhen-Ke Lin,Chung Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 StreamDecisionBench (SDB) 基准，评估流式证据场景下程序内语言模型"即时生效决策"的准确率，并按判断错误与时延错误归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多作为程序内的决策组件，程序根据其返回的决策行动直到新决策到达。当证据在推理期间发生变化时，对旧状态正确的决策可能在其状态已过时仍然生效（如客户开始念卡号后录音仍在继续），而传统离线（无时间）准确率会把这类错误计为正确，因此需要新的评估方法。

Method: SDB 在每个瞬间评估当时生效的决策，并将每个错误瞬间归因于判断、时延或两者；场景覆盖四个应用家族并以流式方式提供证据，参考决策由可执行代码根据公开规则计算。通过 1-5 秒更新间隔下"生效准确率"在对数时间轴上的归一化曲线下面积（AUC）进行汇总，使相等的乘性区间获得相同权重。

Result: 在四个托管模型的六种设置下，该分数与"逐场景的无时间准确率 × oracle 积分时延分数"的乘积相差不超过 2.9 分。推理提升判断能力，但在低努力下时延使 Luna 和 Terra（两个 GPT 模型，也做了无推理评估）相对无时间准确率损失 38.8 和 42.8 分；一个判断较弱但更快的组件在无推理情况下可达到与 Terra 相近的积分分数。聚合与家族曲线显示了这些权衡在哪里发生变化。

Conclusion: SDB 使评估对时间尺度的依赖性变得可见，揭示了判断准确率与时延之间的权衡，并表明推理带来的判断增益可能被时延损失抵消。

Abstract: As natural language drives more applications, language models increasingly run inside programs as decision components: the program sends them the current state and acts on the returned decision until a newer one arrives. When evidence changes during inference, a decision correct for its own state can stay in force after that state has passed, as when a call recorder keeps running after a customer starts reading out a card number; untimed (offline) accuracy counts such an error as correct. We introduce StreamDecisionBench (SDB), which evaluates the decision in force at every instant and attributes every erroneous instant to judgment, latency or both. Its scenarios stream evidence in four application families, with reference decisions computed from public rules by executable code. We summarize in-force accuracy across update intervals of 1-5 s by its normalized area under the curve on a logarithmic time axis, giving equal weight to equal multiplicative ranges. Across six settings of four hosted models, this score stays within 2.9 points of the scenario-wise product of untimed accuracy and an oracle's integrated timing score. Reasoning improves judgment, but at low effort latency costs Luna and Terra, two GPT models we also evaluate without reasoning, 38.8 and 42.8 points relative to untimed accuracy; a faster component with weaker judgment attains a similar integrated score to Terra without reasoning. The aggregate and family curves show where these tradeoffs change, making the evaluation's time-scale dependence visible.

</details>


### [214] [Marking Contour Tones in Yorùbá](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38627)
*Kólá Túbòsún*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对约鲁巴语（Yorùbá）中曲折调在正字法上的长期难题，尤其是人名与词项因传统拼写不标元音延长而导致第二声调无处依附的问题，提出采用caron与circumflex符号标注单辅音上的升调与降调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约鲁巴语是声调语言，曲折调带来持续的正字法挑战。对于人名和某些词项，传统拼写避免元音延长，使得第二个声调失去承载音节；更严重的是，某些姓名的常规拼写不仅省略了声调信息，还会颠倒名字的含义，甚至表达与原名相反的意思。现有解决方案不足以应对这一问题。

Method: 作者首先描述该问题并说明当前方案的不足，随后提出采用caron（ˇ）和circumflex（^）符号作为正字法约定，在单元音上编码升调和降调。这些符号在约鲁巴语音系学研究中自Olmsted（1951）以来已有先例。该方案通过在WriteYoruba键盘和TTSYoruba语音合成器中的实现加以支持。

Result: 该提案使得曲折调首次能够通过标准键盘输入和计算文本处理来访问；相关的键盘与语音合成器架构及听者评估在Tubosun等人（2026）中另行报告。

Conclusion: 采用caron和circumflex标注约鲁巴语单元音上的升调与降调，是一种有学术先例且可在标准键盘和计算文本处理中实现的可行正字法方案，有助于解决人名与词项中曲折调标注不足及语义颠倒的问题。

Abstract: Yorùbá is a tonal language in which contour tones pose persistent orthographic challenges. These are especially notable for personal names and lexical items whose conventional spellings avoid vowel lengthening that would otherwise provide a host syllable for the second tone. A particular concern is a class of names in which the conventional spelling does not just omit tonal information but inverts the meaning of said name, sometimes asserting the opposite of what the name intends. This paper describes the problem, illustrates the inadequacy of current solutions, and proposes the adoption of the caron and circumflex marks. These are symbols with precedent in Yorùbá phonological scholarship since Olmsted (1951), used as orthographic conventions on single vowels to encode rising and falling contour tones, making them accessible for the first time through standard keyboard input and computational text processing. The proposal is supported by an implementation in the WriteYoruba keyboard and the TTSYoruba speech synthesizer, whose architecture and listener evaluation are reported separately (Tubosun et al., 2026).

</details>


### [215] [Strong Multilingual Privacy Tagging at Encoder Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38630)
*Jonathan Graehl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多语言细粒度命名实体标注器，通过前沿模型标注、映射人工金标回放与子词边界修复，实现高效隐私脱敏，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私脱敏需在删除个人信息的同时保留文本中的关系表达，因此需要细粒度、多语言且支持多样化脱敏策略的命名实体标注方法。

Method: 微调多语言编码器，配仿射跨度标注头；用35种语言的前沿模型标注训练，采用覆盖感知掩码回放映射人工金标以避免未标注类型被当作负例，并用学习的±1字符调整修复子词边界。

Result: 在7种语言1,283个金标测试段上，最佳脱敏F1为88.8，远超GLiNER2（69.1）、Presidio（57.3）等；增加5万句训练和人工金标回放使Ont3 F1从74.5升至76.3，映射金标回放单独提升10分，边界调整加1.7分；本地LLM表现不及，编码速度慢30-95倍，编码器CPU吞吐为GLiNER2的4.9倍。

Conclusion: 该方法在多语言细粒度隐私脱敏上取得领先性能，且效率高，并公开代码、提示与训练配方。

Abstract: Privacy redaction must remove personal information while preserving relationships expressed in text. We develop a multilingual named-entity tagger with fine-grained distinctions supporting varied redaction policies and methods for cheaply learning additional distinctions. We fine-tune a multilingual encoder with an affine span-tagging head on frontier-model annotations in 35 languages, replay mapped human gold with coverage-aware masking so unannotated types are not treated as negatives, and repair subword boundaries with a learned +/-1-character adjustment. On 1,283 human-gold test segments in seven languages, best measured redaction F1 is 88.8, against 69.1 for published GLiNER2 with 11 unrepresentable types excluded from its task (68.8 without that exemption), 67.8 for GLiNER2 adapted to the new training data, 57.3 for Microsoft Presidio and 35.8 for the best published OpenAI Privacy Filter fine-tune. Adding about 50,000 annotated training sentences and increasing human-gold replay improves exact typed-span F1 from 74.5 to 76.3 on Ont3, our 31-type frontier-annotated NER evaluation of 1,201 development segments. Mapped-gold replay alone raises human-gold F1 by ten points without loss on frontier-annotated text; boundary adjustment adds 1.7 exact typed-span F1 points on Ont3. Local LLMs fitting on a single 96-GB GPU underperformed as prompted annotators and frozen encoders, with encoding 30-95 times slower than XLM-R inference and prompted annotation roughly 180-1,100 times slower in the evaluated configurations. The encoder architecture delivers 4.9 times GLiNER2's CPU throughput. We release code, prompts and training recipes, with data-acquisition scripts and source links.

</details>


### [216] [MetaSteer: Context-Conditioned, nonlinear Steering via Attention-Projection Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38718)
*Mehdi Jafari,Hao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MetaSteer：学习非线性、上下文相关的干预并作用于注意力投影矩阵，实现零样本迁移的激活引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型引导方法多依赖激活空间中的线性、与上下文无关的干预，这一假设近期受到质疑，且当固定表示需编码多种行为差异时会产生信息瓶颈。

Method: MetaSteer学习具有上下文相关效果的非线性干预，并将其应用于注意力投影矩阵，使激活效果在构造上随输入上下文变化，无需线性概念几何假设；以偏好优化框架训练，在汇总偏好语料上训练一次，即可零样本迁移到未见概念和分布外上下文；使用低秩适配器。

Result: 虽仅用低秩适配器，MetaSteer在隐状态轨迹上诱导出结构化、上下文相关的变化，同时部分保留局部轨迹动力学（如速度与曲率）。在3个受控文本生成基准和3个智能体设置、多模型族与规模上，零样本情形下多数聚合比较中匹配或超越强任务特定引导基线；文本生成引导越强，智能体引导表现也越强。

Conclusion: MetaSteer验证了非线性、上下文相关引导的可行性与迁移性，并讨论了轨迹几何效应、能力保留及可迁移引导带来的安全考量。

Abstract: Steering large language models typically relies on linear, context-independent interventions in activation space, an assumption that recent work has challenged and that can induce an information bottleneck when a fixed representation must encode many behavioral distinctions. We introduce MetaSteer, a method that learns nonlinear interventions with context-dependent effects and applies them to attention projection matrices, producing activation effects that vary with the input context by construction and requiring no linear concept-geometry assumption. Framed as preference-based optimization, MetaSteer is trained once on a pooled preference corpus and transferred zero-shot to unseen concepts and out-of-distribution contexts. We find that, despite using low-rank adapters, MetaSteer induces structured, context-dependent changes in hidden-state trajectories while partially preserving aspects of their local trajectory dynamics, including velocity and curvature. We evaluate MetaSteer on three controlled text-generation benchmarks and three agentic settings across multiple model families and scales. MetaSteer matches or outperforms strong task-specific steering baselines on most aggregate comparisons in the zero-shot regime. Across the evaluated settings, stronger text-generation steering is associated with stronger agentic steering performance. We further discuss geometric trajectory effects, capability retention, and safety considerations raised by transferable steering.

</details>


### [217] [Training LLM Judges from Language Feedback via Position-Selective Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38792)
*Ilgee Hong,Changlong Yu,Zhenghao Xu,Xin Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Qin Lu,Bing Yin,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于自然语言反馈训练LLM评判者，提出利用教师-学生间逐位置熵偏移来识别"上下文锐化"与"上下文扩散"两种模式，并通过位置掩码保留低熵偏移尾部，提升主观任务的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观任务的评判结果强烈依赖所采用的评估标准及其权重，而主流的outcome-supervised RL（如GRPO）仅用最终判定准确率的单一标量对所有token统一给分，无法在标准选择处提供独立信用，也忽略了偏好标签中天然附带的丰富语言反馈（如偏好理由）。

Method: 采用自蒸馏（SD）：同一模型在给定语言反馈条件下作为教师，提供密集的逐位置监督；利用教师与学生间的逐位置熵偏移区分两种机制——"上下文锐化"（教师将概率集中在某个与反馈一致的标准表达上）与"上下文扩散"（教师在多个反馈一致的备选间分配概率）。基于这一不对称性，提出基于熵偏移的位置掩码，保留熵偏移分布的低尾部分，掩掉高熵偏移位置。

Result: 实验结果：掩掉高熵偏移位置相比朴素SD提升了分布外泛化能力；所得自蒸馏评判者在所评估的主观子类上比outcome-supervised RL训练的评判者高出2–9个百分点，同时在客观子类上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 逐位置熵偏移揭示了自蒸馏中"记忆特定标准表达"与"理解语义、保留多种备选"两种机制的差异；选择性地保留低熵偏移位置能更好地促进语义理解，从而提升主观评判任务的泛化性与性能，同时不牺牲客观任务表现。

Abstract: We study training LLM judges from natural language feedback, especially for subjective tasks where the verdict depends strongly on which evaluation criteria the judge invokes and how it weighs them. The dominant approach, outcome-supervised RL (e.g., GRPO), credits every token in the rollout with a single scalar determined only by the accuracy of the final verdict, providing no separate credit at the criterion-choice tokens and ignoring the rich language feedback (e.g., preference rationales) that naturally accompanies preference labels. Self-Distillation (SD) is one natural way to use this language feedback: the same model, conditioned on this feedback, acts as a teacher providing dense, position-level supervision. However, not all positions carry equally useful signal. Using the per-position entropy shift between teacher and student, we identify two regimes: context sharpening, where the teacher concentrates probability on a particular feedback-aligned criterion expression, and context spreading, where the teacher distributes probability across multiple feedback-aligned alternatives. We interpret these patterns as follows: sharpening encourages memorization of a particular criterion expression, whereas spreading promotes semantic understanding by preserving these alternatives. Motivated by this asymmetry, we introduce position masking based on the entropy shift that retains the lower tail of the entropy-shift distribution. Experiments show that masking higher-entropy-shift positions improves out-of-distribution generalization over naive SD. The resulting self-distilled judges outperform judges trained with outcome-supervised RL by 2-9 percentage points on the evaluated subjective subcategories, while remaining competitive on objective ones.

</details>


### [218] [Recovering Off-Policy Supervision for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38795)
*Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Sugyeong Eo,Hyeonseok Moon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于rollout的投机解码草稿模型训练框架，通过Anchor-Label Relabelling (ALR)和In-Rollout Anchors (IRA)两个组件，在不修改原始语料的情况下恢复全部监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的块式草稿模型通常在外部模型生成的语料上训练，一旦块内某个token偏离策略，后续所有位置的监督都会失效。现有方法直接丢弃这些偏移slot，造成严重监督损失。

Method: 提出rollout训练框架，含两个互补组件：(1) ALR用贪心目标rollout的分布替换语料标签，恢复所有预测slot的有效监督；(2) IRA将草稿块直接放入这些rollout中，使草稿模型接触目标生成上下文，并复用预计算的rollout特征，不增加额外目标开销。

Result: 在固定的视觉-语言和文本语料上，相比DFlash贪心接受长度最多提升36.5%，并持续优于erase基线；单轮训练即超过最优erase调度，三轮训练后接受长度可媲美在目标重新生成响应上训练的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为在固定语料上训练投机草稿模型提供了一种有效且计算高效的方法，且无需修改原始文本。代码已开源。

Abstract: Block drafters for speculative decoding are commonly trained on corpora written by external models, where a single off-policy token invalidates supervision for all subsequent slots in a block. Existing approaches discard these divergent slots, resulting in severe supervision loss. To resolve this problem while preserving the training corpus, we propose a rollout-based training framework that recovers full supervision through two complementary components. The first component, Anchor-Label Relabelling (ALR), replaces corpus labels with distributions from greedy target rollouts, restoring valid supervision across all predicted slots. The second component, In-Rollout Anchors (IRA), places draft blocks directly inside these rollouts to expose the drafter to target-generated context, reusing precomputed rollout features at no additional target cost. Across fixed vision-language and text corpora, our framework increases greedy accepted length by up to 36.5% over DFlash and consistently outperforms erasing baselines. Notably, a single epoch of our method surpasses the best erase schedules. After three epochs, it matches the acceptance length of training on target-regenerated responses. These results show that our framework provides an effective and compute-efficient approach for training speculative drafters on fixed corpora without modifying the original text. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/ALR-IRA.

</details>


### [219] [Overlap, Unique and Conflict: Can LLMs Extract What They Can Recognize?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38799)
*Eftekhar Hossain,Santu Karmaker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨叙事任务 OUC 抽取，即从两篇叙事中抽取所有重叠、冲突和独有的子句，并构建约 22K 叙事对、140K 实例的基准，发现 LLM 在重叠与冲突抽取上表现远差于独有信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多视角替代叙事需要识别不同来源信息之间的一致、冲突与差异。已有跨文本关系研究多聚焦于对预定义文本对进行分类（如蕴含或矛盾），而非直接从完整叙事中抽取此类信息，存在明显空白。

Method: 提出 Overlap-Unique-Conflict（OUC）抽取任务，从两篇叙事中抽取所有重叠、冲突和独有子句；构建约 22K 叙事对、140K OUC 实例的基准，覆盖事实性、论证性和政治性话语；评估 14 个开源 LLM（0.6B–35B），并进行诊断分析。

Result: 独有信息抽取远易于重叠和冲突：最强模型 Gemma-4-31B 在重叠上仅 61.13% F1，冲突上 48.58%，独有超过 75%。困难并非仅来自关系识别，而是无法从完整叙事中配对并抽取相应子句，小模型尤为明显。任务特定监督微调可显著缩小差距：微调后的 Qwen-3-8B 较基线提升 15–28 个百分点，并在多项任务上超越约四倍规模的 Qwen-3.6-35B。

Conclusion: 重叠与冲突抽取仍远未令人满意，跨叙事子句抽取仍是一个开放挑战。任务特定监督虽有帮助，但尚不足以解决该问题。

Abstract: Understanding multi-perspective alternative narratives requires identifying how their information agrees, conflicts, or differs across sources. Existing work on cross-text relations largely focuses on categorizing relations between predefined text pairs, such as entailment or contradiction, rather than directly extracting such information from full narratives. To address this gap, we introduce Overlap-Unique-Conflict (OUC) extraction, a cross-narrative task that extracts all overlapping, conflicting, and unique clauses from two narratives. To support this study, we construct a benchmark of approximately 22K narrative pairs and 140K OUC instances spanning factual, argumentative, and political discourse. Evaluating 14 open-source LLMs (0.6B-35B), we find that unique information is far easier to extract than overlap and conflict: the strongest model, Gemma-4-31B, reaches only 61.13% F1-score on overlap and 48.58% on conflict, against more than 75% on unique. Further diagnostic analysis reveals that this difficulty does not stem from relation recognition alone, but rather from a failure to pair and extract the corresponding clauses from full narratives, especially in smaller models. Nevertheless, learning these extractions with task-specific supervision narrows the gap considerably: a fine-tuned Qwen-3-8B gains 15-28% absolute over its baseline and surpasses models roughly four times its size (e.g., Qwen-3.6-35B) on several tasks. Even so, overlap and conflict remain well below satisfactory, leaving cross-narrative clause extraction an open challenge.

</details>


### [220] [Uncovering Uncontrolled Repetition through Residual Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38802)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Xinyao Zhou,Haoran Gao,Yuyao Zhang,Zhenhong Zhou,Fanyu Meng,Li Sun,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Tokenwise Residual Comparison (TRC)方法，通过比较生成过程中注意力与MLP对残差流的写入来定位重复异常，并选择性抑制相关坐标，平均降低57%的循环率，且可泛化到LLMs和LRMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归生成中的不受控重复会延长生成过程并引发资源消耗攻击。已有研究主要识别出中间层和后期层中强烈激活的特征，但对于这些重复活动在变得显著之前如何在早期出现和发展仍缺乏理解。

Method: 作者主要在大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中研究该问题，提出Tokenwise Residual Comparison (TRC)。该方法从生成过程中的残差动态中识别并定位与重复相关的异常：通过比较生成的不同token上注意力与多层感知机（MLP）对残差流的写入来发现重复相关模式，然后在识别出的层上选择性地抑制残差流中的坐标。

Result: 实验表明TRC能有效缓解不受控重复，平均降低57%的循环率。分析显示重复语义在浅层出现并通过残差流传播，扰乱正常表示。TRC也能泛化到大型语言模型（LLMs）和大型推理模型（LRMs），一致地捕捉类似的重复动态并实现有效缓解。

Conclusion: 该工作将重复生成的研究从显著内部表示扩展到更早的干预机会，为缓解资源消耗攻击提供了新见解。

Abstract: Uncontrolled repetition can prolong autoregressive generation in large language models (LLMs) and enable resource consumption attacks. Prior analyses of repetitive generation have identified strongly activated features in intermediate and late layers. However, how uncontrolled repetition activity emerges and develops before becoming prominent in these layers remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we investigate this question primarily in large vision-language models (LVLMs), which support a richer set of uncontrolled repetitions through both visual and textual inputs. We propose Tokenwise Residual Comparison (TRC), a method that identifies and localizes anomalies associated with repetition from residual dynamics during generation. TRC compares attention and multilayer perceptron writes to the residual stream across generated tokens to identify patterns associated with repetition. It then selectively suppresses coordinates in the residual stream at the identified layer. Experiments show that TRC effectively mitigates uncontrolled repetition, reducing loop rates by 57\% on average. Our analysis further shows that repetition semantics emerge in shallow layers and propagate through the residual stream, disrupting normal representations. TRC also generalizes to large language models (LLMs) and large reasoning models (LRMs), where it consistently captures analogous repetition dynamics and achieves effective mitigation. Our work broadens the study of repetitive generation from its prominent internal representations to earlier opportunities for intervention, providing insights for mitigating resource consumption attacks.

</details>


### [221] [StateTree: Enhancing Long-Term Dialogue Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38809)
*Naen Xu,Wanqing Cui,Yibo Hu,Shixin Hong,Hengyu An,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma,Tianyu Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StateTree 提出一种数据驱动的强化学习方法，通过构建树结构路径追踪辅助任务，使模型在 10K 上下文上训练即可泛化到 128K 的长程对话推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化助手需在长且不断演化的交互历史中推理，但相关证据分散在多个会话、偏好随时间变化，而标准长上下文训练在数据稀缺和高算力成本下难以应对这些挑战。

Method: StateTree 从稀缺对话中构建带可验证真值的困难辅助任务：在多会话对话中嵌入键值记录形成二叉树，模型需跨会话检索记录并比较时间戳解决分支、从根遍历到叶，再在干扰叶中恢复隐藏目标问题；采用课程式 RL 逐步增加树深度，并引入边携带步骤级推理片段的组合式变体以训练模型组合部分线索。

Result: 仅用 10K token 上下文训练即泛化到 128K token，无需全长 RL 成本；展现出跨会话检索、时间推理、知识更新与组合式多跳推理能力；优于 SFT 和 RL 基线并保持短上下文通用推理；StateTree-7B 在 LongMemEval (128k) 上最高提升 +23.60%，StateTree-14B 达到 59.00% 准确率，超过 QwenLong-L1-32B (45.20%)。

Conclusion: StateTree 通过可验证的树结构路径追踪任务与课程式 RL，在低成本下赋予模型长程对话推理能力，并能泛化到远超训练长度的上下文。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as personalized assistants must reason over long, evolving interaction histories. However, in long-term dialogue reasoning, relevant evidence is scattered across sessions, preferences may be revised over time, and standard long-context training fails to address these challenges under data scarcity and prohibitive computational costs. We propose StateTree, a data-driven RL method that constructs a challenging auxiliary task from scarce dialogues with verifiable ground truth. StateTree augments multi-session dialogues with a tree-structured path-tracing task: key-value records are embedded across sessions to form a binary tree. Solving the task requires the model to traverse from root to leaf by retrieving records across sessions and comparing timestamps to resolve branches, then recover the hidden target question among distractor leaves. We apply curriculum RL training progressively increasing tree depth and introduce a compositional variant whose edges carry step-level reasoning fragments, training the model to compose partial cues into coherent queries. Trained on 10K-token contexts, StateTree generalizes to 128K tokens without full-length RL costs and exhibits capabilities including cross-session retrieval, temporal reasoning, knowledge update, and compositional multi-hop reasoning. StateTree outperforms both SFT and RL-based baselines while preserving short-context general reasoning. StateTree-7B achieves gains up to +23.60% on LongMemEval (128k), and StateTree-14B reaches 59.00% accuracy on LongMemEval, surpassing QwenLong-L1-32B (45.20%).

</details>


### [222] [Can Terminal Agents Trust Their Own Verification? Diagnosing and Improving Self-Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38812)
*Yingfeng Luo,Shaowei Wei,Daixin Wang,Dingyang Lin,Kaiyan Chang,Weiqiao Shan,Tong Zheng,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断框架量化终端智能体的自验证可靠性，发现错误检测与修复是主要瓶颈，并提出SCVD蒸馏方法来提升验证与恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体依赖自验证来评估和纠正解决方案，但其可信度尚不明确，需要系统性研究其验证与恢复行为。

Method: 构建诊断框架，识别轨迹中首个完整解、判定其客观正确性，并据此量化智能体的验证与恢复行为；基于发现提出Student-Conditioned Verification Distillation (SCVD)，让学生先产出候选解，再从相同交互上下文中蒸馏更强教师的验证与恢复过程。

Result: 在TerminalBench2.1上评估十个终端智能体，发现完整候选形成后验证几乎普遍发生，但仅61.43%的错误候选被检测到，且仅49.36%被检测到的错误被成功修复。在三个Qwen3.5骨架上，SCVD使Pass@1相比基础模型提升9.74–16.85个百分点，相比标准全轨迹蒸馏提升4.49–8.61个百分点，并避免了后者在SWE-bench Verified上的明显分布外退化。

Conclusion: 自验证的主要弱点不在启动验证，而在检测与修复错误；SCVD能有效提升终端智能体的验证与恢复能力。

Abstract: Terminal agents rely on self-verification to assess and correct their solutions as they solve tasks through interaction with command-line environments. Yet how trustworthy such self-verification is remains poorly understood. To investigate this question systematically, we introduce a diagnostic framework that identifies the first complete solution in each trajectory, determines whether it is objectively correct, and uses this ground truth to quantify the agent's subsequent verification and recovery behavior. Applying it to ten terminal agents on TerminalBench2.1, we find that verification is nearly universal after a complete candidate is formed, yet only 61.43\% of incorrect candidates are detected and only 49.36\% of detected errors are successfully repaired. These results show that the main weakness in self-verification lies not in initiating verification, but in detecting and repairing errors. Motivated by these findings, we propose Student-Conditioned Verification Distillation (SCVD), which lets the student first produce a candidate solution and distills a stronger teacher's subsequent verification and recovery from the same interaction context. Across three Qwen3.5 backbones, SCVD improves \textsc{Pass@1} on TerminalBench2.1 by 9.74--16.85 percentage points over the corresponding base models and by 4.49--8.61 points over the standard full-trajectory distillation, while avoiding the pronounced out-of-distribution degradation of full-trajectory distillation on SWE-bench Verified.

</details>


### [223] [You're Hired: Strategic Model Selection for LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38816)
*Zongwan Cao,Ziyuan Yang,Shangbin Feng,Michael Duan,Skyler Hallinan,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多LLM系统中的模型选择问题，提出并评估9种选择算法的分类体系，实验表明有效选择算法比随机或启发式团队最高提升36.1%，其中能力与训练相关的选择策略表现最佳且方差最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体与模型协作算法虽能结合不同大语言模型（LLM）的优势，但系统仍受限于预定义、手工构建的模型池，缺乏对模型选择的系统性研究。

Method: 提出并系统评估一个包含9种选择算法的分类体系，涵盖模型描述的多样性、能力感知的行为多样性以及基于LLM的招募者；在10和32个模型的候选池、4种模型协作算法以及数学、编程、问答、推理等任务上进行大量实验。

Result: 成功的选择算法大幅优于随机或基于启发式的团队（如仅选择个体表现最好的模型），在各设置下最高提升36.1%；基于能力与训练的选择策略可缓解选择方差并取得最佳性能；更大的候选池对浅层启发式选择更具挑战性，而基于与候选模型交互并理解其能力的算法能稳健过滤掉不对齐、不安全的模型，并泛化到新的分布外任务。

Conclusion: 有原则且信息充分的团队选择至关重要，应在此之前部署真实的多LLM系统，本文为构建有效的多LLM系统提供了强有力的模型选择算法。

Abstract: While multi-agent and model collaboration algorithms gain traction to combine the strengths of diverse Large Language Models (LLMs), existing systems remain bottlenecked on pre-defined and hand-crafted model pools. In this work, we investigate the problem of model selection in multi-LLM systems. We propose and systematically evaluate a taxonomy of 9 selection algorithms ranging from diversity of model descriptions, capability-aware behavioral diversity, and LLM-based recruiters. We conduct extensive experiments across two candidate pools of 10 and 32 models, deployed in four model collaboration algorithms, and evaluated across tasks spanning math, coding, QA, and reasoning. Results demonstrate that successful selection algorithms greatly outperform random or heuristics-based teams such as merely selecting the models with top individual performance, by up to 36.1% across settings. Specifically, capability- and training-based selection strategies alleviate selection variance and achieve the best performance, which we recommend to employ before deploying real-world multi-LLM systems. Further analysis reveals that larger candidate pools pose greater challenges to shallow selection heuristics, while algorithms grounded in interacting with candidate models and understanding model capability robustly filter out misaligned, unsafe models, as well as generalizing to novel, out-of-distribution tasks. Together, we establish that principled and informed team selection is critical and present strong model selection algorithms for assembling effective multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [224] [BARRAC: Adaptation of an English Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis Approach for Classification Tasks in Arabic Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38820)
*Ali Almutairi,Gelareh Mohammadi,Imran Razzak,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将英语方面级情感分析框架适配到阿拉伯语分类任务，提出BARRAC两阶段训练方法，在五个阿拉伯语方言数据集上平均macro-F1达63.93%，超越最优少标签SOTA 3%，并在五分之四任务上优于GPT-4o。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语NLP快速发展，已有大量模型、数据集和基准，但研究多集中在资源适配层面。本文探讨核心问题：为英语等主流语言开发的任务方法能否迁移到阿拉伯语任务中，即除适配模型、数据和基准外，任务特定方法的适配也是一条值得探索的路径。

Method: 提出BARRAC（Brainstorming Alignment and Replaced Representation learning for ArabiC tasks）。该方法将消费者评论属性池替换为面向方言情感、讽刺和方言识别的阿拉伯语语言手段与标记；同时用两阶段训练替代带噪的自训练，从而实现对英语方面级情感分析框架的阿拉伯语化改造。

Result: 在五个阿拉伯语方言数据集上评估，BARRAC取得63.93%的平均macro-F1，较最优少标签SOTA高出3%，并在五分之四任务上超过GPT-4o。误差分析进一步揭示了仍存在的挑战。

Conclusion: 结果表明，与适配模型、数据集和基准并行，适配任务特定方法也是阿拉伯语NLP的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of Arabic NLP, several models, datasets and benchmarks have been reported. This paper asks whether approaches developed for majority languages like English can be adapted to Arabic tasks. We adapt an English aspect-based sentiment analysis framework to Arabic classification tasks and present the adaptation as BARRAC: Brainstorming Alignment and Replaced Representation learning for ArabiC tasks. BARRAC replaces consumer-review attribute pools with Arabic linguistic devices and markers for dialectal sentiment, sarcasm, and dialect identification, and replaces noisy self-training with two-stage training. Evaluated on five Arabic dialect datasets, BARRAC achieves a mean macro-F1 of 63.93\%, outperforming the best few-label SOTA by 3\%, and outperforming GPT-4o on four out of five tasks. Error analysis provides insights into remaining challenges. These results demonstrate that adapting task-specific approaches is a promising direction for Arabic NLP alongside adapting models, datasets and benchmarks.

</details>


### [225] [Forging LLM Authorship Fingerprints with Targeted Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38831)
*Haohan Yuan,Simin Chen,Xi Niu,Hanqing Guo,Depeng Xu,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"定向指纹迁移"问题：通过改写某模型的输出，使归属分类器将其误判为指定的目标模型。提出 ForgePrint（先搜索后蒸馏）框架，在摘要任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型归属分类器能在未修改文本上准确识别文本来源，但这并不能说明经过刻意改写后预测是否仍指向原始来源。因此需要研究改写文本能否"迁移"模型指纹，揭示指纹可检测性与真实来源并不等同。

Method: 将问题形式化为定向指纹迁移：改写一个模型的输出，使归属分类器将其判为目标模型。选择摘要任务，因为不同模型处理同一文档、表达同一内容，形成可控的条件生成场景。提出 ForgePrint 框架，采用"先搜索后蒸馏"：先搜索能把归属推向目标指纹的改写，再将其蒸馏进一个单次生成（one-pass）的 4B Student 模型。

Result: 在 CNN/DM 上，Student 达到 70.2% 的目标成功率，优于其 Teacher（54.1%）和六个公开改写基线中最强的一个（39.3%），且攻击从未查询过的留出分类器同样有效。将开源模型摘要迁移到指定商业模型时，目标成功率也达到 68.3%。

Conclusion: 指纹可检测性不应与来源真实性混为一谈。即使在未修改文本上准确，纯文本归属在定向改写下也可能给出误导性的模型身份证据。

Abstract: Model-attribution classifiers can often identify which language model produced a text, making model-specific writing patterns a signal of provenance. Accurate attribution on unmodified text, however, does not show whether the prediction still identifies the original source after deliberate rewriting. We formulate this problem as targeted fingerprint transfer: rewriting one model's output so that attribution classifiers assign it to a chosen target model. We study summarization, where different models receive the same document and express the same underlying content, providing a controlled setting for conditional generation. We introduce ForgePrint, a search-then-distil framework that first searches for rewrites that move attribution toward a target fingerprint, then distils the selected rewrites into a one-pass 4B Student model. On CNN/DM, the Student reaches 70.2% target success rate, outperforming both its Teacher (54.1%) and the strongest of six published rewriting baselines (39.3%), against held-out classifiers that are never queried by the attack. It also reaches 68.3% target success when transferring summaries from an open model toward chosen commercial models. These results show that fingerprint detectability should not be conflated with source authenticity, and that text-only attribution can provide misleading evidence of model identity under targeted rewriting, even when it is accurate on unmodified text.

</details>


### [226] [Scaling Parameter and Context in Attention: Native Sparse Attention from Mixture-of-Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38832)
*Zizhuo Fu,Runsheng Wang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 NAMOH 架构级稀疏注意力：每 token 激活 H 个头中的 K 个，每头仅保留分配 token 并在子序列内做因果注意力，实现参数扩展与上下文扩展的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展注意力参数可提升语言模型质量，但保留完整 token 历史使多头在长上下文下开销高昂；同时注意力本身需检索并组合上下文信息，因此参数扩展也应支持更长上下文。作者由此追问：注意力参数扩展能否直接带来高效且有效的上下文扩展。

Method: 提出 NAMOH：每个 token 激活 H 个头中的 K 个；每个头只保留分配给它的 token，并仅在该子序列内执行因果注意力。头选择同时决定激活参数与可用上下文，无需扫描完整历史。在均衡分配下，固定 K 而增大 H 可缩短各头历史、降低每 token 的 KV 访问量而不增加总 KV 存储。还引入头相对旋转位置编码以缩短路由子序列内的位置跨度，缓解位置引起的注意力噪声。

Result: 实验表明，NAMOH 可优于总参数量相同的全激活模型；在激活参数量与更小稠密模型匹配时，长上下文推理更高效。且与 GQA 及现有稀疏注意力机制兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作为注意力扩展提供新路径，使参数扩展直接支持上下文扩展。

Abstract: Scaling attention parameters can improve language model quality, but retaining full token histories makes additional heads costly at long contexts. Furthermore, since attention retrieves and combines contextual information, parameter scaling should also support longer contexts. We therefore ask whether attention parameter scaling can directly enable efficient and effective context scaling. We introduce NAMOH, an architecture-native sparse attention mechanism that activates $K$ of $H$ heads per token. Each head retains only its assigned tokens and performs causal attention within this subsequence. Head selection thus jointly determines active parameters and available context without scanning the full history. Under balanced assignments, increasing $H$ at fixed $K$ shortens head histories and reduces per-token key-value (KV) access without increasing total KV storage. We further support head-relative rotary position embeddings to shorten positional spans within routed subsequences, aiming to mitigate position-induced attention noise. Experiments show that NAMOH can outperform fully activated models with the same total parameters, while enabling more efficient long-context inference than smaller dense models with matched active parameter counts. It remains compatible with GQA and existing sparse attention mechanisms. We hope this work offers a new path for scaling attention, with parameter scaling directly enabling context scaling.

</details>


### [227] [Where MLLMs Fail and Why: Causal Task Decomposition for Capability Failure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38851)
*Xia Hu,Brian Potetz,Chun-Ta Lu,Huanfen Yao,Leonidas Guibas,Zhicheng Wang,Howard Zhou,Pengfei Xing,Andrew Gallagher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出因果分解框架，通过控制任务前置依赖干预，将MLLM组合任务失败区分为内在能力缺陷与级联错误，并实例化为10个组合任务、46个单元任务、3.3万+问题的CADET诊断基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端准确率只能记录MLLM在组合任务上的失败频率，无法判断失败源于目标能力的内在缺陷，还是上游前置能力的级联错误，导致诊断信息不足。

Method: 提出因果分解框架，对每个任务的前置依赖进行受控干预。用能力指标（NC、IC、RC）分别在无辅助、正确前置、错误前置条件下评分；用贡献指标（N-Score、S-Score）量化各前置的必要性与充分性；并构建CADET基准，包含感知、空间、时间、认知四类的10个组合任务、46个单元任务和33,000+人工标注问题。

Result: 诊断前沿MLLM发现端到端准确率掩盖的系统模式：在认知任务上提供正确前置可消除54%的错误，使其从最弱类别升至空间和时间之上；因果贡献集中于少数关键前置，提供单个最重要前置即可获得提供全部前置收益的84%。

Conclusion: 因果分解框架能揭示MLLM组合失败的成因结构，区分能力缺陷与级联错误，为定向改进提供依据。

Abstract: End-to-end accuracy on compositional tasks records how often MLLMs fail, but cannot distinguish whether a failure reflects an intrinsic deficit in the targeted capability or a cascading error from an upstream prerequisite. We propose a causal decomposition framework that isolates these two failure modes through controlled interventions on the prerequisite dependencies of each task. Our capability metrics (NC, IC, RC) score each task under unassisted, correct, or incorrect prerequisites to diagnose where failures arise; contribution metrics (N-Score, S-Score), adapted from probabilities of causation, quantify each prerequisite's necessity and sufficiency to determine why. We instantiate the framework in CADET, a diagnostic benchmark of 10 composite tasks decomposed into 46 unit tasks with over 33,000 human-annotated questions spanning perception, spatial, temporal, and cognitive categories. Diagnosing frontier MLLMs with our framework uncovers systematic patterns that end-to-end accuracy obscures. Capability-wise, supplying correct prerequisites eliminates 54\% of errors on cognitive tasks, lifting them from weakest to above spatial and temporal. Prerequisite-wise, causal contributions are concentrated in a few critical prerequisites, and supplying the single most important one alone captures 84\% of the gain from supplying all prerequisites.

</details>


### [228] [TRACE: Target-Aware Retrieval, Attributed Evidence, and Contract-Constrained Extraction for LitTraceQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38861)
*Sachin Gupta,Divya Godara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE系统通过目标分组检索、独立定位证据、多模态表格提取与失败关闭校验，弥合“来源可访问”与“评估可见正确性”之间的grounding contract gap，在官方71题测试集上取得0.760613分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 找到相关论文并不等于能据此产生可验证的答案。LitTraceQA要求规范的论文标识符、页面或对象级的精确证据，以及与被评估器匹配的类型化答案，而来源访问与评分可见正确性之间存在“grounding contract gap”。

Method: TRACE（Target-Aware Retrieval, Attributed Evidence, Contract-Constrained Extraction）采用目标分组检索、独立类型化证据定位、多模态表格提取、模式驱动表格构建与失败关闭校验；对27,487篇论文建立段落、对象、别名、引用与稠密表示索引并保留问题目标；表格方面先预测观察单元再抽取数值，并用评估器兼容的键归一化组装行。

Result: 经审计的清洁赛道产物在官方71题测试集上得分0.760613，其中论文F1 0.9728、证据F1 0.6847、多选题准确率0.9800、表格行F1 0.5423、宏单元准确率0.3508；在11条公开开发集表格记录上，清洁基线与坐标感知视觉填充的行F1分别为0.291和0.411（含回退输出，非官方测试结论）。

Conclusion: TRACE在检索、证据定位与表格抽取上取得较强表现，剩余错误主要集中在定位器、观察单元、行键与来源值身份识别方面。该诊断性比较不构成官方测试主张。

Abstract: Finding a relevant paper is not the same as producing a verifiable answer from it. LitTraceQA requires canonical paper identifiers, exact evidence at the page or object level, and typed answers that match the evaluator. We call the separation between source access and scorer-visible correctness the grounding contract gap. TRACE - Target-Aware Retrieval, Attributed Evidence, and Contract-Constrained Extraction - addresses this gap with target-grouped retrieval, independent typed evidence localization, multimodal table extraction, schema-driven table construction, and fail-closed validation. It indexes 27,487 papers through passage, object, alias, citation, and dense representations while retaining the question target behind each signal. For tables, TRACE predicts the observation unit before extracting values and assembles rows with evaluator-compatible key normalization. Our audited selected clean-track artifact scores 0.760613 on the official 71-question test set, including 0.9728 paper F1, 0.6847 evidence F1, 0.9800 multiple-choice accuracy, 0.5423 table-row F1, and 0.3508 macro cell accuracy. On 11 public-development table records, a clean baseline and coordinate-aware visual fill obtain row F1 of 0.291 and 0.411, respectively; this diagnostic comparison includes fallback outputs and is not an official-test claim. Remaining errors chiefly concern locator, observation-unit, row-key, and source-value identity.

</details>


### [229] [GraphForge: Training Working Agents with Graph-Anchored Workspace Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38923)
*Qisheng Su,Hanchen Wang,Guanru Zhu,Huicheng Jiang,Qiuyinzhe Zhang,Kou Shi,Zhen Fang,Ziao Zhang,Qingnan Ren,Zehui Chen,Tao Gui,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GraphForge，一个基于证据图的框架，将任务构建与验证都锚定在真实文件上，用合成的 2169 条轨迹微调 Qwen3.6-27B 后在多个智能体基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练能读取多样文件、协调工具并产出交付物的智能体，需要大量基于真实文件且结果可验证的任务。现有数据合成流程存在两种缺陷：一是用模型生成文件，缺乏真实性与多样性；二是基于真实文件构建任务却没有针对任务的验证器，导致结果质量无法保证。因此需要一种能兼顾真实文件与可验证性的任务合成框架。

Method: GraphForge 以职业相关的种子作为起点以保证可控多样性，为每个种子组装由真实文件构成的工作空间，并基于文件间关系构建证据图。任务陈述与评分标准（rubrics）均从该证据图导出，使任务要求有工作空间文件作支撑，每条评分标准都锚定到验证所需的文件。随后进行初始 rollout 以测试可执行性，并由修订智能体对照原始文件修复任务与评分标准，之后再收集轨迹。在 SFT 模型自身 rollout 上还进行了拒绝微调，用证据锚定的评分标准筛选候选。

Result: 在 OpenHands 下，用 2169 条 GraphForge 轨迹微调 Qwen3.6-27B 使 GDPVal 达到 1445.7（+65.7）；在 Claude Code 下，Workspace-Bench-Lite 与 SpreadsheetBench II 分别达到 63.7（+7.7）和 24.0（+13.7）。在 SFT 模型自身 rollout 上做拒绝微调，并以证据锚定评分标准筛选候选，在三个基准上均带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 证据锚定的评分标准可作为有效的选择信号，GraphForge 能同时保证真实文件基础与可验证性。数据和模型已公开。

Abstract: Working agents need to read diverse files, coordinate tools, and produce deliverables. Training such agents requires tasks built on many real files with verifiable results, but few pipelines exist to synthesize this kind of data. Existing pipelines either generate files with models, which lack realism and diversity, or build tasks on real files without task-specific verifiers, leaving result quality unchecked. We introduce GraphForge, an evidence-graph based framework that grounds both the task and its verification in real files. Starting from occupation-grounded seeds for controlled diversity, GraphForge assembles a workspace of real files for each seed and builds an evidence graph over their relations. Since the task statement and rubrics are both derived from this graph, task requirements are backed by the workspace files and each criterion is anchored to the files needed to verify it. An initial rollout further tests executability, and a revision agent repairs the task and rubrics against the original files before trajectories are collected. Fine-tuning Qwen3.6-27B on 2,169 GraphForge trajectories brings GDPVal to 1445.7 (+65.7) under OpenHands, and Workspace-Bench-Lite and SpreadsheetBench II to 63.7 (+7.7) and 24.0 (+13.7) under Claude Code. Rejection fine-tuning on the SFT model's own rollouts, with candidates selected by the evidence-anchored rubrics, yields further improvements on all three benchmarks, suggesting that the rubrics provide a useful selection signal. The data and models are available.

</details>


### [230] [Making LLMs Say What They Think: Measuring and Improving CoT-Interpretability Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38972)
*Yihuai Hong,Shauli Ravfogel,Chen Zhao,Eunsol Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CIA指标衡量大模型推理链（CoT）与内部计算的一致性，发现对齐度有限，并通过后训练显著提升忠实性同时保持或提高任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT常被视为LLM得出答案过程的代理，但越来越多证据表明CoT往往无法反映模型内部计算，甚至可以在不影响最终答案的情况下被改变，因此需要衡量并提升CoT与内部推理的一致性。

Method: 提出CoT-Interpretability Alignment (CIA)指标，衡量CoT轨迹与可解释性工具检测到的内部推理策略之间的一致性；在三个任务（两跳问答、提示干预、整数乘法）和三个LLM上评估，并通过后训练（将任务准确率与参数化忠实性信号作为奖励）来改进CIA。

Result: LLM在所有任务上的对齐度有限（44.8%-75.9%）；后训练可以大幅提升CoT参数化忠实性，同时保持或提高任务准确率，并给出了泛化模式等分析。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了审计CoT参数化忠实性的框架，以及使模型显式推理更可信的路径，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) traces often serve as a proxy for how Large Language Models (LLMs) arrive at their answers. However, growing evidence shows that models' CoT often fails to reflect their internal computations and can be changed without affecting their final answers. In this work, we measure and improve the alignment between the reasoning described in an LLM's CoT and what it computes internally. We propose CoT-Interpretability Alignment (CIA), a metric that measures the agreement between a model's CoT traces and its internal reasoning strategies as detected by interpretability tools. We evaluate CIA on three tasks (two-hop question answering, hint intervention, and integer multiplication) across three LLMs, finding that LLMs exhibit limited alignment across all tasks (44.8-75.9%). We then experiment with improving CIA via post-training, setting both the task accuracy and parametric faithfulness signals as a reward. Experiments show that we can substantially improve CoT parametric faithfulness while maintaining or improving the task accuracy. We provide rich analysis, such as their generalization patterns. Our work provides both a framework for auditing CoT parametric faithfulness and a pathway toward making models' explicit reasoning more trustworthy. Code and data are available at https://github.com/yihuaihong/CIA-minimal-repro.

</details>


### [231] [Fairness Beyond a Single Run: Training-Seed Variability in Speech LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38976)
*Srishti Ginjala,Eric Fosler-Lussier,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在语音LLM微调中，随机种子对人口统计公平性指标的影响通常大于音频压缩因子，单次训练的公平性报告不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别的人口统计公平性差距几乎总是基于单次训练运行来报告，但这类单次结果是否能代表稳定的公平性表现并不清楚，因此需要系统考察随机种子与音频压缩等因素对公平性指标的相对贡献。

Method: 固定编码器、基础解码器、数据与解码策略，对语音LLM的Q-former投影器和LoRA适配器进行微调；设置5个音频压缩因子与6个随机种子，在Common Voice与Fair-Speech上评估每个运行；采用平衡的3x3分解量化种子与压缩对公平性变异的贡献，并控制准确率与dropout、将适配数据扩展至960小时进行验证。

Result: 在460小时干净LibriSpeech上，多数人口统计轴上种子对公平性指标的影响大于压缩；Fair-Speech族群归一化差距的变异中85.3%归因于种子，仅8.3%归因于压缩（p=0.009），而压缩在年龄和性别上解释更多；不同种子的LibriSpeech词错误率仅波动0.04个百分点，Common Voice波动8.57，说明并非失败运行；扩展到960小时可减弱但未消除该效应；在Fair-Speech族群上，两个单次系统需归一化差距相差超过0.30才能超出种子变异。

Conclusion: 单次训练报告的公平性差距可能主要反映随机种子噪声而非模型或压缩设定差异，因此公平性评估应报告多种子变异性，并谨慎解释单次系统间的公平性比较。

Abstract: Demographic fairness gaps in automatic speech recognition are almost always reported from a single training run. We fine-tune the Q-former projector and LoRA adapters of a speech LLM at five audio compression factors and six random seeds, holding the encoder, base decoder, data and decoding fixed, and evaluate every run on Common Voice and Fair-Speech. At 460 h of clean LibriSpeech, the seed moves fairness metrics more than compression does on most demographic axes. A balanced 3x3 decomposition attributes 85.3% of the variation in Fair-Speech ethnicity normalized gap to the seed against 8.3% to compression (p = 0.009), though compression explains more on age and gender. Held-out LibriSpeech word error rate spreads by 0.04 points across those seeds while Common Voice spreads by 8.57, so these are not failed runs, and the effect survives controlling for accuracy and dropout. Scaling and diversifying the adaptation set to 960 h damps the effect but does not remove it. On Fair-Speech ethnicity, two single-run systems must differ by more than 0.30 in normalized gap to exceed seed variability.

</details>


### [232] [When Clipping Reverses Correction: Failure Dynamics of Pointwise Forward-KL On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38995)
*Di Huang,Hao Li,Yixin Chen,Fuhai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 on-policy self-distillation(OPSD)中广泛采用的 forward KL 逐点截断技巧,发现该截断反而导致学生模型产生难以终止的重复,并从理论上证明截断目标可能将 token 概率推离教师分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 OPSD 研究提出逐点截断可稳定训练,后续工作普遍沿用,但其对训练的实际影响缺乏直接检验。作者希望厘清该截断是否真正有益。

Method: 设计仅在校验是否施加截断这一点上不同的匹配训练实验,对比有无截断时模型生成重复的程度,并结合数学证明分析截断目标函数对学生与教师概率关系的影响,再用训练过程中的概率统计加以验证。

Result: 施加截断的训练出现明显更多、且持续到回答末尾的重复;理论上证明截断目标既可能无法把学生拉向教师,又可能同时把被截断与未截断 token 的概率推离教师。实验统计与理论一致:重复段落内,截断的学生比教师更少给出退出重复的概率、更多给出继续重复的概率。

Conclusion: 逐点截断并非无害的稳定手段,它会诱发顽固重复;应当重新评估 OPSD 中该截断的必要性与替代方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own generated responses using feedback from the same model conditioned on privileged information. On mathematical reasoning, the original OPSD study finds that stylistic tokens can dominate the training signal over math-related tokens, and that pointwise clipping of the forward KL objective stabilizes training. Pointwise clipping caps each vocabulary-wise forward KL term at a fixed threshold before summing over the vocabulary. Follow-up studies have adopted this clipping, but its effect on training has not been directly examined. In matched training runs differing only in whether clipping is applied, we observe that clipped runs produce substantially more repetitions that persist to the end of the response than their unclipped counterparts. We trace this failure to the clipped objective. We prove that the clipped objective can fail to correct the student toward the teacher and can instead push clipped and unclipped token probabilities away from its teacher. Our training runs agree with this analysis: inside repetitions, the clipped student places less probability than its teacher on leaving the repetition, and more on continuing it, whereas the unclipped runs stay close to their teachers.

</details>


### [233] [Settle: Learning When to Stop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38997)
*Ryan Brown,Zihao Fu,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Settle 提出一种通过学习已完成推理轨迹中答案稳定性来判断停止时机的方法，在推理模型上显著减少冗余生成 token 且几乎不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型常在答案已经确定后仍继续生成冗余内容，造成计算浪费；现有停止方法难以在准确率与 token 数量之间取得良好折中。

Method: Settle 从已完成的推理轨迹中学习答案稳定性，以此判断何时停止；训练已有的“推理结束”token，同时保持其他预测接近基座模型，推理时仅需普通解码。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 与 MATH-500 上，Settle 将 token 数减少 40%，准确率仅下降 0.5 个百分点；相比在同一批轨迹首个稳定答案处截断后做监督微调，准确率高 6.16 个百分点，而 token 数几乎相同；其停止分数还能预测正确答案是否会保持正确，并扩展了所评估停止方法的准确率—token 数帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: Settle 能有效利用答案稳定性实现推理停止，在保持准确率的同时大幅压缩生成长度，并优于监督微调等基线方法。

Abstract: Reasoning models often continue generating after their answers have settled. Settle learns when to stop from answer stability in completed traces. It trains the existing end-of-reasoning token while keeping other predictions close to the base model, and requires only ordinary decoding at inference. On MATH-500 with Qwen3-4B, Settle reduces token count by 40% with a 0.5-percentage-point decrease in accuracy. It gains 6.16 percentage points over supervised fine-tuning on the same traces shortened at their first stable answer, at nearly identical token counts. Its stopping score predicts whether a correct answer will remain correct. Settle extends the accuracy-token-count Pareto frontier of the evaluated stopping methods.

</details>


### [234] [The Invisible Language Tax: Token Premiums of French and Regional Languages in 2026 LLM Tokenizers, and a French-Optimized Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39001)
*Thomas Serval*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测量了2026年主流模型分词器在不同语言上的"token溢价"：法语比英语多耗31%–58%的token，法国地区及海外语言达英语的1.6–3.3倍；通过BPE控制实验证明加入法语训练数据可快速降低溢价但存在收益递减与英语成本上升，并提出原型分词器Baracoda FR v1.2，在法语上比Tekken少用11.5%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务按token计费、上下文窗口也以token衡量，但相同内容在不同语言下所需token数差异显著。这种差异会造成非英语用户（尤其是法语及法国地区语言使用者）在费用与可用上下文上的隐性不公平，因此需要量化该溢价并探索缓解方案。

Method: 在NTREX-128（124种非英语参考译文）和《世界人权宣言》地区语言版本上，对七款2026年常用模型的分词器（OpenAI o200k、Llama 3、Qwen3、DeepSeek V3/V4、Gemma 3、Mistral Tekken及经Anthropic计数API的Claude第5代分词器）测量token溢价；开展BPE控制实验（Europarl、50k词表）考察加入法语的影响；并构建字节级BPE原型Baracoda FR v1.2，采用Tekken词表规模，在预先固定协议下用六个设计阶段未接触的语料进行测试，并做训练数据重叠剔除与等普通token预算的稳健性检验。

Result: 法语比英语多需31%–58%的token；简体中文为少5%到多40%，且在七款分词器中的六款上比法语更省；法国地区与海外语言约为英语的1.6–3.3倍。控制实验显示加入法语数据可快速降低溢价，但收益递减且英语成本上升。Baracoda FR v1.2在法语上比Tekken少用11.5%的token、英语少用3.7%，结果在剔除重叠句与等普通token预算下依然成立；但在其他语言上更差，且在可比词表规模下未优于CroissantLLM。

Conclusion: 多语言token溢价是真实且可量化的，且会因历史重发、分层定价与固定上下文窗口在智能体场景下被放大。分词器层面可通过补充训练数据或专门设计部分缓解法语溢价，但存在权衡（英语成本、其他语言表现）。本文结论仅限于分词（切分）结果，对模型质量与任务成本的影响仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: LLM services are billed per token and context windows are measured in tokens, yet the number of tokens needed for the same content varies across languages. We measure this token premium on seven tokenizers of widely used 2026 models (OpenAI o200k, Llama 3, Qwen3, DeepSeek V3/V4, Gemma 3, Mistral Tekken, and the Claude generation-5 tokenizer via Anthropic's counting API) on NTREX-128 (124 non-English reference translations) and on the Universal Declaration of Human Rights for regional languages. French requires 31% to 58% more tokens than English, whereas Simplified Chinese ranges from 5% fewer to 40% more and is cheaper than French on six of the seven tokenizers. Regional and overseas languages of France pay roughly 1.6 to 3.3 times the English count. We discuss how history re-sending, tiered pricing and fixed context windows amplify the absolute gap in agentic use. In a controlled experiment (BPE, Europarl, 50k vocabulary), adding French to tokenizer training data quickly reduces the premium, with diminishing returns and a growing cost for English. Finally, we present Baracoda FR v1.2, a byte-level BPE prototype with Tekken's vocabulary size. On a final test of six corpora never consulted during design, with a protocol declared fixed beforehand, it uses 11.5% fewer tokens than Tekken on French and 3.7% fewer on English; results hold after removing test sentences overlapping the training data and with an equal ordinary-token budget. It is worse on other languages and, at comparable vocabulary size, does not outperform CroissantLLM. These are segmentation results only; effects on model quality and task cost remain to be shown.

</details>


### [235] [Evidence First, Arithmetic Second: A System Report and Failure Analysis for DocSem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39013)
*Divya Godara,Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EVICALC系统在DocSem共享任务最终测试中取得8.61%联合准确率，公开验证集上达92.17%答案准确率和1.00证据F1，两者配置不同不可直接比较，错误分析为描述性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向DocSem共享任务开发一套从PDF中定位证据段落并完成算术推理的系统，并对其失败情况进行可复现、可检查的分析。

Method: EVICALC读取PDF，选取相关段落，调用语言模型生成算术表达式，再在本地代码中执行求值；保存中间结果以支持失败检查。另开展读取页面图像的探索性研究（100份文档）。

Result: 官方最终测试：1,730个任务上联合准确率8.61%；公开验证运行：答案准确率92.17%、证据F1为1.00。人工事后分析发现一例OCR与块分组将相关段落并入其他块，导致系统基于无关文本作答；页面图像探索研究中100份文档仅22份返回证据标识符。

Conclusion: 两组分数因配置与指标不同而非受控对比；描述性发现仅用于推动后续评估，不能确立整体得分的成因。

Abstract: EVICALC, our system for the DocSem shared task, achieved 8.61% joint accuracy on 1,730 tasks in the official final test evaluation. It reads a PDF, selects a passage, asks a language model to write an arithmetic expression, and evaluates that expression in local code. Saved intermediate results support inspection of failures. A separate public-validation run achieved 92.17% answer accuracy and 1.00 evidence F1. The configurations and metrics differ, so these scores are not a controlled comparison. Our manual, post-hoc analysis is descriptive: in one inspected case, optical character recognition (OCR) and block grouping merged the relevant passage into another block, and the system answered from unrelated text. An exploratory study of reading page images on 100 documents returned evidence identifiers for only 22 documents. These descriptive findings motivate further evaluation; they do not establish the causes of the overall score.

</details>


### [236] [A Missing Piece for Trustworthy AI Reviewers: From Benchmarking Rhetorical Robustness to SciCore Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39027)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Chengrui Fan,Jianpeng Chen,Han Chen,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"修辞鲁棒性"概念，构建RobustReview基准（1260个稿件版本）评估30种审稿配置，揭示"虚假鲁棒性"现象，并提出双分支审稿模型SciCore以降低修辞敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审稿系统可能对同一科学内容的不同措辞给出不同判断，从而奖励修辞优化而非科学改进。人类对齐与修辞鲁棒性对审稿系统的排名不一致，需要将修辞鲁棒性作为独立的评估目标。

Method: 提出修辞鲁棒性为"内容保持改写下的稳定性"与"论文间区分度"的联合要求。构建RobustReview全稿件受控基准，包含1260个稿件版本，评估30种审稿配置。提出SciCore双分支审稿器，将对全文的判断与对提取的结构化"科学核心"的判断进行平均。

Result: 基准揭示"虚假鲁棒性"现象，即低改写敏感性与论文间评分崩塌同时出现；人类对齐与修辞鲁棒性对审稿器的排名不同；内容聚焦提示协议不能稳定提升各骨干模型的鲁棒性。在GPT-5.5对比中，SciCore取得领先的联合稳定性-区分度表现，同时保持有竞争力的人类对齐。

Conclusion: 修辞鲁棒性可作为独立的评估目标被确立，基于"科学核心"的审稿设计在提升修辞鲁棒性方面具有潜力，为AI审稿系统评估提供新维度。

Abstract: AI reviewers can assign different judgments to manuscripts that report the same science in different wording, potentially rewarding rhetorical optimization over scientific improvement. We formulate Rhetorical Robustness as the joint requirement of stability across content-preserving rewrites and discrimination across papers. We introduce RobustReview, a controlled full-manuscript benchmark with 1,260 manuscript versions, and evaluate 30 reviewer configurations. The benchmark reveals false robustness, where low rewrite sensitivity coincides with score collapse across papers, and shows that human alignment and rhetorical robustness rank reviewers differently. Moreover, the evaluated content-focused prompting protocol does not consistently improve robustness across backbones. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SciCore, a dual-branch reviewer that averages a full-manuscript judgment with a judgment based on an extracted, structured science core. This design combines manuscript-level assessment with a content-normalized view intended to reduce rhetorical sensitivity. In our primary GPT-5.5 comparison, SciCore achieves a leading joint stability-discrimination profile among the benchmarked reviewers while maintaining competitive human alignment. These results identify rhetorical robustness as a distinct evaluation target and demonstrate the potential of science-core review to improve it.

</details>


### [237] [RSIGame: Autonomous Agentic Game Development with Recursive Self-improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39045)
*Wenyi Wu,Minghao Fu,Jieyu You,Kun Zhou,Siqi Liu,Aayush Salvi,Yiheng Lin,Ce Zhang,Xiaohan Lan,Jiahui Zhu,Yujie Zhong,Qi She,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RSIGame：一种具备递归自我改进能力的自主智能体游戏开发框架，通过局部与全局双循环迭代提升生成游戏质量，并将开发经验内化进生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使自动游戏生成变得可行，但仅停留在"可玩"层面仍难以可靠地进一步提升质量。朴素的迭代精修容易在小规模测试用例上过拟合，导致游戏脆弱、bug 未解决、行为缺失，且难以泛化到更广泛的玩家交互。

Method: RSIGame 将开发过程组织为互补的局部与全局循环。局部"探索—诊断—改进"循环：广泛探索可执行游戏，诊断并排序所发现问题，进行有证据支撑的修改，并用不断演化的检查清单持续积累新的测试与改进指引。全局循环：跟踪整体质量，保留最佳检查点，检测长时程开发中的饱和或退化。此外，在测试时改进之外，还通过训练将成功开发经验内化到生成器中。

Result: 在 140 个 GameCraft-Bench 任务、两种游戏引擎、五种生成器上，RSIGame 在同等开发预算下持续提升游戏质量。经验内化使 Qwen3.8-27B 在 Godot 上达 61.38、Phaser 上达 58.53，超过 GPT-5.5 一次性生成得分，同时将 Qwen 的生成 token 减少 11 倍。

Conclusion: 递归自我改进的智能体框架能有效突破游戏生成的质量瓶颈，且经验内化可同时提升质量并大幅降低生成成本，为自主游戏开发提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made automatic game generation increasingly feasible, yet reliably improving generated games beyond a playable version remains challenging. Naive iterative refinement can easily overfit a small set of test cases, producing fragile games with unresolved bugs, missing behaviors, and poor generalization to broader player interactions. We introduce RSIGame, an autonomous agentic game development framework with recursive self-improvement. RSIGame organizes development into complementary local and global loops. Concretely, a local explore-diagnose-improve loop broadly explores the executable game, diagnoses and prioritizes discovered issues, and performs evidence-grounded revision, where an evolving checklist continually accumulates new testing and improvement guidance. A global loop tracks overall quality, preserves the best checkpoint, and detects saturation or regression over long-horizon development. Beyond test-time improvement, RSIGame further internalizes successful development experience into the generator through training. Across 140 GameCraft-Bench tasks, two game engines, and five generators, RSIGame consistently improves game quality under matched development budgets. Notably, experience internalization enables Qwen3.8-27B to reach 61.38 on Godot and 58.53 on Phaser, exceeding GPT-5.5 one-shot scores while reducing Qwen's generation tokens by 11 times.

</details>


### [238] [Structure vs. Chain-of-Thought: Evaluating LLM Criteria Extraction for Depression Severity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39049)
*Xinkai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文比较了"直接评分"与"先标注临床标准再由代码换算"两种抑郁症严重度评估方法，发现在有序一致性（二次加权kappa）上，标准抽取相较思维链通常无显著优势，且一致性高并不意味着更能检出重度病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准抽取法更易审计（临床医生可逐条核查），因此需要实证检验它是否真的优于直接提示与思维链，并在不同语料、模型规模与问卷下验证其稳健性。

Method: 在两个Reddit语料上，用三个规模从9B到前沿的LLM，针对PHQ-9与BDI-II两套问卷，比较直接评分、思维链与临床标准抽取三种方式，以二次加权kappa衡量与标签的一致性。分别测试阈值在标注数据上拟合、先验固定以及思维链在同一批标签上重校准的情形，并统计重度病例的漏检情况。

Result: 对两个前沿模型，标准抽取仅在阈值于标注数据上拟合时，才在一个语料上超过思维链，且增益不显著；在与思维链同标签重校准后仍不显著。阈值按PHQ-9先验固定时，抽取在两个语料上均无增益，即便模型每帖标注了超过两个标准。9B模型在抑郁社区语料上表现不同：无论直接提示还是思维链都倾向判为重度，而先验规则在无标签下优于两者；思维链同标签重校准后差距不再显著，符合校准效应。但更高的有序一致性并不保证更好检出重度病例：PHQ-9标准抽取漏掉大多数重度帖，且从直接提示到思维链再到抽取，几乎所有比较中漏检都在增加。在主要语料（一个重标注的压力数据集）上，使用该数据集自身特征（含文本词数）的模型与先验规则下的前沿标准抽取无显著差异。

Conclusion: 标准抽取在有序一致性上的优势有限且不稳健，其表面收益多可归因于阈值校准；高一致性不等于对临床关键的重度病例有更好识别，反而漏检更多。评估应同时关注严重度检出等临床相关指标，而非仅看一致性。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) can rate depression severity directly from a social media post or mark which clinical criteria the post shows and let code turn the count into a label. The latter is easier to audit because a clinician can check each marked criterion. We compare these approaches on two Reddit corpora using three LLMs (from 9B to frontier scale) and two questionnaires (PHQ-9, BDI-II), and measure agreement with quadratic weighted kappa. For the two frontier models, criteria extraction scores above chain-of-thought on one corpus only when its decision thresholds are fitted on labeled data. Neither model's gain is significant, with or without recalibrating chain-of-thought on the same labels. With thresholds fixed a priori from PHQ-9's criteria, extraction shows no gain on either corpus, even where models mark over two criteria per post. The 9B model behaves differently on a corpus from depression communities. It labels most posts severe, whether prompted directly or with chain-of-thought, while the a priori rule beats both without labels. After chain-of-thought is recalibrated on the same labels, no significant gap remains, consistent with a calibration effect. Yet higher ordinal agreement does not ensure better detection of severe cases. PHQ-9 criteria extraction misses most severe posts, and moving from direct prompting to chain-of-thought and then to extraction increases misses in nearly all comparisons. On the primary corpus, a relabeled stress dataset, a model using that dataset's own features, including word counts from the text, is not significantly different from frontier criteria extraction under the a priori rule.

</details>


### [239] [CORE: Conflict-Oriented Reasoning Elimination for Verifiable Language-Model Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39069)
*Siyu Song,Rui Xu,Jia Lin,Kai Liu,Weifang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出搜索控制器CORE，利用验证器提供的经验证的冲突核进行回跳与冲突缓存，避免重复错误；在图上着色与多种推理任务上均优于时序修复与思维树方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时推理系统在失败时通常重启或仅修正最近一步，即使错误的根源在于更早的决策，导致效率低且可能重复犯错，因此需要利用失败解释来指导搜索方向。

Method: CORE从验证器请求经验证的冲突核，回跳到该核中最近的一个决策点，并缓存该冲突以避免重复；论文给出在可靠验证、有限分支与深度、穷尽提议条件下无上限搜索的完备性且不剪枝有效解的理论保证。

Result: 在2,000个植入式图着色实例上，CORE相比时序修复将中位验证器调用数在30变量时降低39.8%、36变量时降低35.0%，且缓存在单独回跳基础上进一步提升；在五个推理任务上，CORE平均成功率达75.9%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）与84.2%（Qwen3-8B），高于思维树的72.5%与81.8%，且验证器调用与生成token更少。

Conclusion: 使用经验证的失败解释来引导语言模型搜索是有效的，CORE在正确性保证、搜索效率与多任务推理表现上均具优势。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning systems often respond to failure by restarting or revising the latest step, even when an earlier decision caused the error. We introduce CORE, a search controller that requests a certified conflict core from a verifier, backjumps to the latest decision in that core, and caches the conflict to avoid repeating it. Under sound verification, finite branching and depth, and exhaustive proposals, the uncapped search is complete and never prunes a valid solution. On 2,000 planted graph-coloring instances with matched proposals and an exact verifier, CORE reduces median verifier calls by 39.8% at 30 variables and 35.0% at 36 variables relative to chronological repair; caching further improves on backjumping alone. Across five reasoning tasks, CORE achieves 75.9% mean success with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and 84.2% with Qwen3-8B, compared with 72.5% and 81.8% for Tree of Thoughts. It also uses fewer verifier calls and generated tokens on both backbones. These results show the value of using certified failure explanations to direct language-model search.

</details>


### [240] [LexReward: A Taxonomy-Driven Reward Framework for Legal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39071)
*Yida Cai,Xin Dai,Bingxiang He,Huiyuan Xie,Yuxiao Ye,Zhenghao Liu,Yang Bai,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LexReward，一个基于分类体系的法律奖励建模框架，从风格(Style)、要素(Element)和推理链(Chain)三个维度评估法律回答质量，并基于此构建偏好数据用于DPO与奖励模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律语言模型的奖励方法多依赖粗粒度的整体判断，缺乏领域特异性和可解释性，难以捕捉法律回答的多维质量。

Method: 提出三维度分类体系：Style(词汇与句法质量)、Element(法律主体、事实、法条与裁决)、Chain(推理的顺序、完整性、正确性与非冗余性)；为每个维度制定评分标准(rubrics)；据此构建成对偏好数据，用于DPO和奖励模型(LexRM)训练。

Result: 实验表明基于评分标准的奖励能可靠区分不同质量的法律回答；用偏好数据做DPO训练在三个维度上均有提升；各维度专用奖励模型可通过强化学习提升对应维度的策略表现，且奖励时无需参考答案。

Conclusion: 维度化分析与实验共同验证了该分类体系与奖励构建方法的有效性，为法律奖励建模提供了更具领域特异性和可解释性的方案。

Abstract: Legal language models require reward signals that capture not only answer correctness but also the multidimensional quality of legal responses. Existing reward methods, however, often rely on coarse-grained holistic judgments, providing limited domain specificity and interpretability. We introduce LexReward, a taxonomy-driven framework for legal reward modeling. LexReward characterizes legal response quality along three complementary dimensions: Style, covering lexical and syntactic quality; Element, assessing legal subjects, facts, statutes, and decisions; and Chain, evaluating the order, completeness, correctness, and non-redundancy of legal reasoning. For each dimension, we develop rubrics that specify evaluation criteria and quality levels. The resulting rewards are used to construct pairwise preference data for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and reward-model training. Experiments show that the rubric-based rewards reliably distinguish legal responses of different quality and that DPO training on the preference data improves performance across all three dimensions. The learned reward models, LexRM, also support effective downstream optimization: each dimension-specific reward model improves policy performance in its corresponding dimension through reinforcement learning, without requiring reference answers at reward time. Dimension-wise analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed taxonomy and reward construction.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Text: LLM-Based Dimensional Emotion Evaluation in Multimodal Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39072)
*Yutong Hu,Jinho Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于LLM的多模态对话情感评估框架，在IEMOCAP上同时进行离散情感识别与VAD维度评估，通过LoRA微调实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话情感识别研究虽多，但将大语言模型应用于多模态对话的连续维度情感评估尚属空白，且缺乏对提示工程与微调策略的系统对比。

Method: 将声学线索按SpeechCueLLM方式转为自然语言描述，构建LLM框架在IEMOCAP上做离散情感识别与VAD评估；对LLaMA、GPT、Qwen三个家族共六个模型，在零样本提示、少样本提示和LoRA微调三种设定下评测，并开展消融实验。

Result: LoRA微调的LLaMA显著优于提示工程的GPT，差距源于领域适应而非模型容量；最佳模型Valence CCC达0.7822，创IEMOCAP新SOTA；文本音频描述对小模型提升明显（加权F1 +3.5~3.6），对最大模型帮助有限；VAD各维度性能差异与IEMOCAP标注者一致性层级高度吻合。

Conclusion: 领域适应比模型规模更关键；音频线索在语言能力受限时价值最大；情感维度评估难度内在反映标注一致性，为多模态连续情感评估提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversation has been widely studied, but applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to continuous dimensional emotion evaluation in multimodal dialogue remains largely unexplored. We propose an LLM-based framework that performs discrete emotion recognition and Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) dimensional evaluation on IEMOCAP, incorporating acoustic cues as natural language descriptions following the SpeechCueLLM approach. We evaluate six models spanning the LLaMA, GPT, and Qwen families under zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, and LoRA fine-tuning. LoRA fine-tuned LLaMA models substantially outperform prompt-engineered GPT models on both tasks despite GPT's larger scale, a gap we attribute to domain adaptation rather than model capacity. Our best model achieves a Valence CCC of 0.7822, a new state-of-the-art on IEMOCAP. Ablation studies confirm that textual audio descriptions meaningfully improve smaller models (+3.5 to 3.6 weighted F1) while contributing little for the largest model, suggesting audio cues are most valuable when linguistic capacity is limited. The performance asymmetry across VAD dimensions closely mirrors the annotator agreement hierarchy in IEMOCAP's own annotations.

</details>


### [242] [False Frontiers: Diagnosing and Mitigating Co-Cheating in Self-Evolving Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39102)
*Meijia Chen,Hao Li,Zheng Lu,Hongshan Lin,Junbai Tian,Yichen Liu,Zijun Tian,Yufan Zou,Shuhan Sun,Hanxin Chen,Zeyu Zhang,Weizhi Du,Yueting Li,Tianyu Shi,Alaa Khamis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文识别了自演化搜索智能体中的"共同作弊"（co-cheating）失效模式，并提出多采样验证（MSV）与主方法CrossFit来缓解，CrossFit在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化搜索智能体通过联合优化出题器（proposer）与解题器（solver）构建训练课程，但这一闭环会导致双方在共享错误上达成一致，即"共同作弊"：内部奖励提升却没有带来外部正确性的相应增益。作者希望通过后验审计揭示这一问题的严重性，并寻求有效缓解方法。

Method: 首先提出多采样验证（MSV），对同一模型分别在有源文档和无源文档条件下各查询三次，以决定任务准入并替换不可靠的伪标签。但MSV仅部分缓解且成本高，故提出主方法CrossFit：将出题器的源文档分为A、B两组，A生成的问题由仅在B上训练的辅助解题器评分，反之亦然；交叉拟合的一致性决定出题器奖励，使同源伪标签无法通过反馈解题器复现，而原解题器更新规则保持不变。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B和9B上重跑循环，MSV将虚假一致质量从6.1%降至5.7%、8.8%降至7.2%；CrossFit进一步降至3.0%和3.7%。用排除源的反馈重放相同提议可将虚假一致降至0.4%和0.1%，从而将反馈来源与课程变化分离。在七个下游搜索基准上，CrossFit相较标准耦合自演化平均提升8.8和8.4分，相较Search-R1提升8.7和7.8分（4B和9B）。

Conclusion: 共同作弊随自演化轮次递增而加剧，MSV成本高且残留较多，CrossFit通过交叉拟合有效切断反馈来源，显著降低虚假一致并大幅提升下游搜索任务性能。

Abstract: Self-evolving search agents build their own training curricula by jointly optimizing a proposer that generates questions and a solver that answers them. This closed loop introduces a failure mode we call co-cheating: the proposer and solver increasingly agree on shared errors, so internal reward improves without a matching gain in external correctness. A post-hoc audit against source evidence shows co-cheating growing more severe over successive rounds of self-evolution, with pseudo-label correctness stagnating or declining even as the in-loop training signal improves. The most direct mitigation is to verify proposals before training: we introduce multi-sample verification (MSV), which queries the same model three times with the source and three times without it to decide task admission and replace unreliable pseudo-labels. MSV partially reduces false agreement but leaves substantial residual co-cheating and costs six extra labeler generations per candidate. These limitations motivate CrossFit, our main method: it partitions the proposer's source documents into groups A and B; questions generated from A are scored by an auxiliary solver trained only on B, and vice versa. The cross-fitted agreement determines proposer reward, so a same-source pseudo-label cannot be reproduced through the feedback solver, while the original solver's update rule is unchanged. Rerunning the loop with Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3.5-9B, MSV reduces false-agreement mass from 6.1% to 5.7% and from 8.8% to 7.2%, whereas CrossFit reduces it to 3.0% and 3.7%. Replaying identical proposals with source-excluded feedback further reduces false agreement to 0.4% and 0.1%, isolating feedback ancestry from curriculum changes. Across seven downstream search benchmarks, CrossFit improves average performance over standard coupled self-evolution by 8.8 and 8.4 points and over Search-R1 by 8.7 and 7.8 points at 4B and 9B.

</details>


### [243] [Bongard: Training Machine Intuition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39111)
*Li Ding,Haidi Jin,Chen Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Bongard，一个开源权重的"系统一"直觉模型，将机器直觉作为可独立设计训练的能力，通过表征学习与结果反馈系统性训练，实现高效高吞吐决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类智能高度依赖习得的直觉：识别模式、判断情境而无需展开每一步中间推理。作者希望把机器直觉当作一种可独立设计、训练的能力来构建，为高吞吐决策任务提供开放、高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用 T5Gemma 2 4B-4B 编码器-解码器架构，将"读证据"与"作判断"分离：编码器双向读取状态与问题指令，多个解码器分支共享该编码，使同一状态的多次判断只需读取一次；训练好的判别头直接输出候选概率而无需生成文本。训练分三阶段（监督判断→语义关系→动作结果），每阶段在单张 Blackwell GPU 上更新全部 70.9 亿可训练参数；并采用联合嵌入后训练与沙盒阶段（从动作轨迹和精确预言机学习结果分布）。

Result: 联合嵌入后训练将留出改写样本准确率从 75.7% 提升至 85.9%；沙盒阶段将冻结沙盒面板准确率从 50.6% 提升至 64.8%。在 DecisionBench 上，最终模型在 23,900 个决策上达到 78.05%，在 61 个系统的公开对比中排名第四。单张 RTX PRO 6000 上，短请求中位延迟 36 ms，关于同一状态的 32 个问题耗时 221 ms。

Conclusion: Bongard 表明机器直觉可通过表征学习与结果反馈被系统性训练，为高吞吐决策负载提供了开放、高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Human intelligence relies heavily on learned intuition: recognising patterns and judging situations without explicitly unfolding every intermediate step. We introduce Bongard, an open-weight System One model that treats machine intuition as an independent capability to design and train. A T5Gemma 2 4B-4B encoder-decoder separates reading the evidence from making judgments. The encoder reads the state bidirectionally together with the question instructions, and separate decoder branches share this encoding, so many judgments about the same situation require only one reading of the state. A trained head returns probabilities over the supplied candidates without generating text. Training proceeds in three stages, from supervised judgments to semantic relationships to action outcomes, and each stage updates all 7.09 billion trainable parameters on one Blackwell GPU. Joint-embedding post-training raises accuracy on held-out rephrasings from 75.7% to 85.9%. A sandbox stage then learns outcome distributions from action rollouts and exact oracles, raising accuracy on a frozen sandbox panel from 50.6% to 64.8%. On DecisionBench, the final model reaches 78.05% accuracy over 23,900 decisions and ranks fourth of 61 systems in the public comparison. On one RTX PRO 6000, its median latency is 36 ms for short requests, and 32 questions about one state take 221 ms. Bongard demonstrates that machine intuition can be systematically trained via representation learning and outcome feedback, providing an open, efficient alternative for high-throughput decision workloads.

</details>


### [244] [Diagnosing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39118)
